    s = s & "    h = h & ""  ZWSP  (U+200B)  Zero-Width Space: completely invisible; affects word break."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  ZWNJ  (U+200C)  Zero-Width Non-Joiner: controls ligature/joining behaviour."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  ZWJ   (U+200D)  Zero-Width Joiner: forces joined rendering."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  BOM   (U+FEFF)  Byte Order Mark: encoding marker; never belongs in text."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  SHY   (U+00AD)  Soft Hyphen: invisible optional hyphen."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  NBHY  (U+2011)  Non-Breaking Hyphen: a hyphen that prevents line breaks."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights matches in yellow without changing anything."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Invisible Unicode Cleaner""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UpdateCustomVisibility()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraCustom.Visible = optCustom.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ClearCustomChecks()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNBSP.Value = False: chkZWSP.Value = False: chkZWNJ.Value = False: chkZWJ.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkBOM.Value = False: chkSoftHyphen.Value = False: chkNBHyphen.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdSelectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkNBSP.Value = True: chkZWSP.Value = True: chkZWNJ.Value = True: chkZWJ.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkBOM.Value = True: chkSoftHyphen.Value = True: chkNBHyphen.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdDeselectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function UnicodeChar(ByVal codePoint As Long) As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If codePoint > &H7FFF Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        UnicodeChar = ChrW$(codePoint - &H10000)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        UnicodeChar = ChrW$(codePoint)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Unicode Cleaner"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim findList As Collection, replaceList As Collection, itemLabels As Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim counts() As Long, idx As Long, i As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set findList = New Collection: Set replaceList = New Collection: Set itemLabels = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAll.Value Or optNBSP.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&HA0): replaceList.Add "" "" : itemLabels.Add ""Non-breaking spaces (U+00A0)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAll.Value Or optZeroWidth.Value Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H200B): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""Zero-width spaces (U+200B)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H200C): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""Zero-width non-joiners (U+200C)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H200D): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""Zero-width joiners (U+200D)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        findList.Add UnicodeChar(&HFEFF): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""BOM / zero-width no-break spaces (U+FEFF)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAll.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&HAD): replaceList.Add ""-"" : itemLabels.Add ""Soft hyphens (U+00AD)"" : findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H2011): replaceList.Add ""-"" : itemLabels.Add ""Non-breaking hyphens (U+2011)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optCustom.Value Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set findList = New Collection: Set replaceList = New Collection: Set itemLabels = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If chkNBSP.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&HA0): replaceList.Add "" "" : itemLabels.Add ""Non-breaking spaces (U+00A0)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If chkZWSP.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H200B): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""Zero-width spaces (U+200B)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If chkZWNJ.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H200C): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""Zero-width non-joiners (U+200C)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If chkZWJ.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H200D): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""Zero-width joiners (U+200D)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If chkBOM.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&HFEFF): replaceList.Add """" : itemLabels.Add ""BOM / zero-width no-break spaces (U+FEFF)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If chkSoftHyphen.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&HAD): replaceList.Add ""-"" : itemLabels.Add ""Soft hyphens (U+00AD)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If chkNBHyphen.Value Then findList.Add UnicodeChar(&H2011): replaceList.Add ""-"" : itemLabels.Add ""Non-breaking hyphens (U+2011)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If findList.Count = 0 Then MsgBox ""No Unicode options selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ReDim counts(1 To findList.Count): For i = 1 To findList.Count: counts(i) = 0: Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For idx = 1 To findList.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = findList(idx): .Replacement.Text = ""^&"": .Replacement.Highlight = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Forward = True: .Wrap = wdFindStop: .MatchWildcards = False: .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next idx" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Unicode Cleaner"", ""Preview complete. Matches highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Unicode Cleaner""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Unicode Cleaner""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For idx = 1 To findList.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = findList(idx): .Replacement.Text = replaceList(idx): .Forward = True: .Wrap = wdFindStop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): counts(idx) = counts(idx) + 1: Wend" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next idx" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting targetRange" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For idx = 1 To findList.Count: results.Add itemLabels(idx) & "": "" & counts(idx) & "" replaced"": Next idx" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Unicode Cleanup"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmUnicodeCleanup_Code = s
End Function

' Spacing cleanup (full implementation)
Private Function frmSpacingCleanup_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Spacing Fixer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Corrects spacing inconsistencies throughout the document: collapses" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  double (and multiple) spaces, trims leading and trailing spaces from" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  paragraphs, removes spaces before punctuation, normalizes spaces after" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  punctuation to a single space, and collapses runs of excess blank lines." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.GroupName = ""SpacMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optDoubleSpaces.GroupName = ""SpacMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optTrim.GroupName = ""SpacMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.GroupName = ""SpacMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""SpacScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""SpacScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Value = True: UpdateCustomVisibility: ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Caption = ""All spacing fixes""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optDoubleSpaces.Caption = ""Double spaces only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optTrim.Caption = ""Trim leading / trailing spaces""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.Caption = ""Custom (choose below)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkDoubleSpaces.Caption = ""Collapse double (and multiple) spaces""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkTrimSpaces.Caption = ""Remove leading and trailing spaces""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkSpaceBeforePunct.Caption = ""Remove space before punctuation  ( , . : ; ! ? )""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizeAfterPunct.Caption = ""Normalize spacing after punctuation""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkExtraBlankLines.Caption = ""Collapse extra blank lines between paragraphs""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Spacing Fixer"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Corrects the most common spacing inconsistencies in assembled documents."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Double spaces       --  Collapses two or more consecutive spaces to one."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Trim paragraphs     --  Removes leading and trailing spaces from each"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                          paragraph."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Space before punct  --  Removes any space immediately before . , ; : ! ?"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Space after punct   --  Ensures exactly one space follows  . ? ! , ; :"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Extra blank lines   --  Collapses three or more consecutive blank"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                          paragraphs down to two."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Each option counts how many changes it makes and reports them at the end."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights problem areas without making changes."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Spacing Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UpdateCustomVisibility(): fraCustom.Visible = optCustom.Value: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ClearCustomChecks()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkDoubleSpaces.Value = False: chkTrimSpaces.Value = False: chkSpaceBeforePunct.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizeAfterPunct.Value = False: chkExtraBlankLines.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdSelectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkDoubleSpaces.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkTrimSpaces.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkSpaceBeforePunct.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkNormalizeAfterPunct.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkExtraBlankLines.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdDeselectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Spacing Fixer"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doDouble As Boolean, doTrim As Boolean, doBeforePunct As Boolean, doAfterPunct As Boolean, doBlank As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAll.Value Then doDouble = True: doTrim = True: doBeforePunct = True: doAfterPunct = True: doBlank = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optDoubleSpaces.Value Then doDouble = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optTrim.Value Then doTrim = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optCustom.Value Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        doDouble = chkDoubleSpaces.Value: doTrim = chkTrimSpaces.Value: doBeforePunct = chkSpaceBeforePunct.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        doAfterPunct = chkNormalizeAfterPunct.Value: doBlank = chkExtraBlankLines.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doDouble Or doTrim Or doBeforePunct Or doAfterPunct Or doBlank) Then MsgBox ""No spacing options selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Highlight preview matches" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doDouble Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Text = ""  """ & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Text = ""^&""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Highlight = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBeforePunct Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Text = "" ([.,;:!?])""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .MatchWildcards = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Text = ""^&""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Highlight = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .MatchWildcards = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doAfterPunct Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim punctPatterns: punctPatterns = Array("".  "",""?  "",""!  "","",  "","";  "","":  "")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim punct: For Each punct In punctPatterns: With targetRange.Find: .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = punct: .Replacement.Text = ""^&"": .Replacement.Highlight = True: .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll: End With: Next punct" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBlank Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Text = ""^p^p^p""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Text = ""^&""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Highlight = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doTrim Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Text = ""^p """ & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Text = ""^&""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Highlight = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Text = "" ^p""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Replacement.Text = ""^&""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Spacing Fixer"", ""Preview complete. Matches highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Spacing Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Spacing Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Perform replacements with counting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntDouble As Long, cntTrim As Long, cntBefore As Long, cntAfter As Long, cntBlank As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doDouble Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Text = ""  "": .Replacement.Text = "" "": .Forward = True: .Wrap = wdFindStop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Do While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): cntDouble = cntDouble + 1: Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ' Mop-up pass: a triple-space becomes a double-space after the loop above; repeat until clean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .Text = ""  """ & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .Replacement.Text = "" """ & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Not .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceAll) Then Exit Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Loop While targetRange.Find.Execute(FindText:=""  "", Forward:=True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doTrim Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim para As Paragraph" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each para In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If para.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And para.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim paraText As String: paraText = para.Range.Text" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Right$(paraText, 1) = vbCr Then paraText = Left$(paraText, Len(paraText) - 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim trimmedText As String: trimmedText = Trim$(paraText)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If trimmedText <> paraText Then para.Range.Text = trimmedText & vbCr: cntTrim = cntTrim + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next para" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBeforePunct Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find: .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = "" ([.,;:!?])"": .Replacement.Text = ""\1"": .MatchWildcards = True: Do While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): cntBefore = cntBefore + 1: Loop: .MatchWildcards = False: End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doAfterPunct Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim punctPatterns: punctPatterns = Array("".  "",""?  "",""!  "","",  "","";  "","":  "")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim pattern: For Each pattern In punctPatterns: With targetRange.Find: .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = pattern: .Replacement.Text = Left$(pattern, 1) & "" "": .MatchWildcards = False: Do While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): cntAfter = cntAfter + 1: Loop: End With: Next pattern" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBlank Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find: .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = ""^p^p^p"": .Replacement.Text = ""^p^p"": Do While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): cntBlank = cntBlank + 1: Loop: End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .Text = ""^p^p^p""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .Replacement.Text = ""^p^p""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Not .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceAll) Then Exit Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Loop While targetRange.Find.Execute(FindText:=""^p^p^p"", Forward:=True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting targetRange" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doDouble      Then results.Add ""Double spaces removed:         "" & cntDouble" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doTrim        Then results.Add ""Leading/trailing trims:        "" & cntTrim" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBeforePunct Then results.Add ""Spaces before punctuation:     "" & cntBefore" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doAfterPunct  Then results.Add ""Spaces after punctuation fixed: "" & cntAfter" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBlank       Then results.Add ""Extra blank lines collapsed:    "" & cntBlank" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Spacing Cleanup"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmSpacingCleanup_Code = s
End Function

' Capitalization cleanup (full implementation)
Private Function frmCapitalizationCleanup_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Capitalization Fixer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Applies consistent capitalization across paragraphs: sentence case," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  title case, UPPERCASE, lowercase, or smart sentence detection (which" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  capitalizes the first letter after sentence-ending punctuation)." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Operates paragraph by paragraph within the target scope." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.GroupName = ""CapMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optSentence.GroupName = ""CapMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optTitle.GroupName = ""CapMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optUpper.GroupName = ""CapMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optLower.GroupName = ""CapMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.GroupName = ""CapMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""CapScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""CapScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Value = True: UpdateCustomVisibility: ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Caption = ""All capitalization fixes""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optSentence.Caption = ""Sentence case""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optTitle.Caption = ""Title case""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optUpper.Caption = ""ALL CAPS -> Title Case""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optLower.Caption = ""lowercase -> Sentence case""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.Caption = ""Custom (choose below)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkSentence.Caption = ""Capitalize sentence starts""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkTitle.Caption = ""Apply title case to headings""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkUpper.Caption = ""Convert ALL-CAPS words (5+ letters)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkLower.Caption = ""Fix all-lowercase paragraphs""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkSmartSentences.Caption = ""Smart sentence detection (skip abbreviations)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Capitalization Fixer"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Applies consistent capitalisation to every paragraph in the target scope."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""MODES"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  All (Smart)   --  Default. Capitalises the first letter after sentence-ending"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                    punctuation (. ? !).  Best choice for general text."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Sentence case --  First letter of paragraph capitalised; rest lowercased."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Title Case    --  First letter of every word capitalised."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  UPPERCASE     --  Every letter uppercased."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  lowercase     --  Every letter lowercased."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Custom        --  Combine modes using the checkboxes.  If more than one "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""is selected, each is applied in sequence -- the last"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                    one applied wins."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: Capitalisation is applied to the full text content of each paragraph, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""including any directly formatted runs within it."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights paragraphs that would change."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Capitalization Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UpdateCustomVisibility(): fraCustom.Visible = optCustom.Value: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ClearCustomChecks(): chkSentence.Value = False: chkTitle.Value = False: chkUpper.Value = False: chkLower.Value = False: chkSmartSentences.Value = False: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdSelectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkSentence.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkTitle.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkUpper.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkLower.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkSmartSentences.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdDeselectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Capitalization Fixer"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doSentence As Boolean, doTitle As Boolean, doUpper As Boolean, doLower As Boolean, doSmart As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAll.Value Then doSmart = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optSentence.Value Then doSentence = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optTitle.Value Then doTitle = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optUpper.Value Then doUpper = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optLower.Value Then doLower = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optCustom.Value Then doSentence = chkSentence.Value: doTitle = chkTitle.Value: doUpper = chkUpper.Value: doLower = chkLower.Value: doSmart = chkSmartSentences.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doSentence Or doTitle Or doUpper Or doLower Or doSmart) Then MsgBox ""No capitalization option selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Preview: highlight paragraphs that would change (simple heuristic)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, original As String, transformed As String, changedCount As Long: changedCount = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            original = p.Range.Text" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Right$(original, 1) = vbCr Then original = Left$(original, Len(original) - 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            transformed = original" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doSentence Then transformed = SentenceCase(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doTitle Then transformed = TitleCase(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doUpper Then transformed = UCase$(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doLower Then transformed = LCase$(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doSmart Then transformed = SmartSentenceCase(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If transformed <> original Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: changedCount = changedCount + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Capitalization Fixer"", ""Preview complete. "" & changedCount & "" paragraphs highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Capitalization Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Capitalization Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Apply changes" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            original = p.Range.Text" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Right$(original, 1) = vbCr Then original = Left$(original, Len(original) - 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            transformed = original" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doSentence Then transformed = SentenceCase(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doTitle Then transformed = TitleCase(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doUpper Then transformed = UCase$(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doLower Then transformed = LCase$(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doSmart Then transformed = SmartSentenceCase(transformed)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If transformed <> original Then p.Range.Text = transformed & vbCr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting targetRange" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    results.Add ""Paragraphs changed: "" & changedCount" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Capitalization Cleanup"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' Helper functions for capitalization" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function SentenceCase(s As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    s = LCase$(s)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Len(Trim$(s)) = 0 Then SentenceCase = s: Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim leadLen As Long: leadLen = Len(s) - Len(LTrim$(s))" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    SentenceCase = Left$(s, leadLen) & UCase$(Mid$(s, leadLen + 1, 1)) & Mid$(s, leadLen + 2)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function TitleCase(s As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim arr: arr = Split(LCase$(s), "" "")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim i As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Len(arr(i)) > 0 Then arr(i) = UCase$(Left$(arr(i), 1)) & Mid$(arr(i), 2)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    TitleCase = Join(arr, "" "")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function SmartSentenceCase(s As String) As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Very simple heuristic: capitalize after sentence-ending punctuation" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim i As Long, result As String, capNext As Boolean: capNext = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For i = 1 To Len(s)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim currentChar As String: currentChar = Mid$(s, i, 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If capNext And currentChar >= ""a"" And currentChar <= ""z"" Then currentChar = UCase$(currentChar): capNext = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        result = result & currentChar" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If currentChar = ""."" Or currentChar = ""?"" Or currentChar = ""!"" Then capNext = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    SmartSentenceCase = result" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmCapitalizationCleanup_Code = s
End Function

' List normalizer (full implementation)
Private Function frmListCleanup_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  List Normalizer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Normalizes list formatting: converts hyphen/asterisk lines to proper" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Word bullet lists, standardizes existing bullet styles, converts" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  manually-typed numbered lists (1. 2. 3.) to Word auto-numbering," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  and fixes list indentation to the standard 0.25-inch depth." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.GroupName = ""ListMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optBullets.GroupName = ""ListMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optNumbering.GroupName = ""ListMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optIndent.GroupName = ""ListMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.GroupName = ""ListMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""ListScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""ListScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Value = True: UpdateCustomVisibility: ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Caption = ""All list fixes""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optBullets.Caption = ""Convert manual bullet lists""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optNumbering.Caption = ""Convert manual numbered lists""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optIndent.Caption = ""Fix list indentation""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.Caption = ""Custom (choose below)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizeBullets.Caption = ""Convert manual hyphens / asterisks to List Bullet style""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizeNumbering.Caption = ""Convert manual numbers to List Number style""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFixIndent.Caption = ""Fix list indentation""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHyphenToBullets.Caption = ""Convert hyphen-dash lines to bullets""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""List Normalizer"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Standardises list formatting throughout the document."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Normalise bullets    --  Resets all bullet-list paragraphs to the default"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                           Word bullet style, removing inconsistent characters."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Normalise numbering  --  Converts manually typed numbered paragraphs"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                           (e.g.  1. Item) to proper Word auto-numbering."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Fix list indentation --  Sets list paragraph indentation to 0.25 inch."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Convert hyphens      --  Converts lines beginning with '- ' or '* ' to"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                           proper Word bullet-list paragraphs."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""TIP: Run 'Convert hyphens' first if you have many hyphen-lists, then "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""'Normalise bullets' to make them all consistent."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights list paragraphs that would be affected."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  List Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UpdateCustomVisibility(): fraCustom.Visible = optCustom.Value: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ClearCustomChecks(): chkNormalizeBullets.Value = False: chkNormalizeNumbering.Value = False: chkFixIndent.Value = False: chkHyphenToBullets.Value = False: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdSelectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkNormalizeBullets.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkNormalizeNumbering.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkFixIndent.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkHyphenToBullets.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdDeselectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""List Normalizer"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doBullets As Boolean, doNumbering As Boolean, doIndent As Boolean, doHyphen As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAll.Value Then doBullets = True: doNumbering = True: doIndent = True: doHyphen = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optBullets.Value Then doBullets = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optNumbering.Value Then doNumbering = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optIndent.Value Then doIndent = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optCustom.Value Then doBullets = chkNormalizeBullets.Value: doNumbering = chkNormalizeNumbering.Value: doIndent = chkFixIndent.Value: doHyphen = chkHyphenToBullets.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doBullets Or doNumbering Or doIndent Or doHyphen) Then MsgBox ""No list options selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Preview: highlight candidate paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, txt As String, changed As Long: changed = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        txt = Trim$(p.Range.Text)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Right$(txt, 1) = vbCr Then txt = Left$(txt, Len(txt) - 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doHyphen And (Left$(txt, 2) = ""- "" Or Left$(txt, 2) = ""* "") Then p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: changed = changed + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doNumbering And txt Like ""[0-9]*.*"" Then p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: changed = changed + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doBullets And (Left$(txt, 2) = Chr(149) & "" "" Or Left$(txt, 2) = ""* "") Then p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: changed = changed + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""List Normalizer"", ""Preview complete. "" & changed & "" paragraphs highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""List Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - List Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntHyphen As Long, cntBullets As Long, cntNumbering As Long, cntIndent As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doHyphen    Then cntHyphen    = ConvertHyphenListsToBullets(targetRange)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBullets   Then cntBullets   = NormalizeBullets(targetRange)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doNumbering Then cntNumbering = NormalizeNumbering(targetRange)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doIndent    Then cntIndent    = FixListIndentation(targetRange)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting targetRange" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doHyphen    Then results.Add ""Hyphen lists converted to bullets: "" & cntHyphen" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doBullets   Then results.Add ""Bullet styles normalized:          "" & cntBullets" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doNumbering Then results.Add ""Numbered items normalized:         "" & cntNumbering" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doIndent    Then results.Add ""List indents fixed:                "" & cntIndent" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""List Normalization"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ConvertHyphenListsToBullets(scopeRange As Range) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, txt As String, cnt As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= scopeRange.Start And p.Range.End <= scopeRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            txt = Trim$(p.Range.Text)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Right$(txt, 1) = vbCr Then txt = Left$(txt, Len(txt) - 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Left$(txt, 2) = ""- "" Or Left$(txt, 2) = ""* "" Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                p.Range.Text = Mid$(txt, 3) & vbCr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                p.Range.ListFormat.ApplyBulletDefault" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ConvertHyphenListsToBullets = cnt" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function NormalizeBullets(scopeRange As Range) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, cnt As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= scopeRange.Start And p.Range.End <= scopeRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If p.Range.ListFormat.ListType <> wdListNoNumbering Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If p.Range.ListFormat.ListType = wdListBullet Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    p.Range.ListFormat.ApplyBulletDefault" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    NormalizeBullets = cnt" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function NormalizeNumbering(scopeRange As Range) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, txt As String, cnt As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= scopeRange.Start And p.Range.End <= scopeRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            txt = Trim$(p.Range.Text)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Right$(txt, 1) = vbCr Then txt = Left$(txt, Len(txt) - 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If txt Like ""[0-9]*.*"" Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                p.Range.ListFormat.ApplyNumberDefault" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim pos As Long: pos = InStr(txt, ""."" )" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If pos > 0 Then p.Range.Text = LTrim$(Mid$(txt, pos + 1)) & vbCr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    NormalizeNumbering = cnt" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function FixListIndentation(scopeRange As Range) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, cnt As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= scopeRange.Start And p.Range.End <= scopeRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If p.Range.ListFormat.ListType <> wdListNoNumbering Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                p.LeftIndent = InchesToPoints(0.25)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    FixListIndentation = cnt" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmListCleanup_Code = s
End Function

' Paragraph structure cleanup (full implementation)
Private Function frmParagraphCleanup_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Paragraph Structure Fixer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Cleans up paragraph structure: removes empty paragraphs, collapses" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  runs of three or more paragraph breaks down to two, resets" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  SpaceBefore/SpaceAfter to zero for paragraphs with direct spacing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  overrides, and resets left indent to zero." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.GroupName = ""ParaMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optRemoveEmpty.GroupName = ""ParaMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optNormalizeSpacing.GroupName = ""ParaMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.GroupName = ""ParaMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""ParaScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""ParaScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Value = True: UpdateCustomVisibility: ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAll.Caption = ""All paragraph fixes""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optRemoveEmpty.Caption = ""Remove empty paragraphs""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optNormalizeSpacing.Caption = ""Normalize paragraph spacing""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optCustom.Caption = ""Custom (choose below)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveEmpty.Caption = ""Collapse multiple consecutive empty paragraphs""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkCollapseBreaks.Caption = ""Convert soft returns (Shift+Enter) to paragraph marks""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizeParaSpacing.Caption = ""Normalize Space Before / Space After settings""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFixIndent.Caption = ""Remove manual tab / space indentation at paragraph start""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Paragraph Structure Fixer"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Cleans up paragraph structure and direct spacing overrides."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove empty paragraphs  --  Deletes completely empty paragraphs."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Collapse extra breaks    --  Reduces runs of 3+ consecutive paragraph breaks"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                               to exactly 2 (a standard visual separator)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Normalise para spacing   --  Resets SpaceBefore and SpaceAfter to zero for"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                               paragraphs that have direct spacing overrides."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                               Does not affect spacing set by the style itself."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Fix indent               --  Resets left indent to zero for paragraphs with"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                               direct indent overrides."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""TIP: Run after assembling a document from multiple sources or templates, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""where inconsistent spacing between paragraphs is most common."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights empty paragraphs before any changes are made."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Paragraph Structure Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UpdateCustomVisibility(): fraCustom.Visible = optCustom.Value: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ClearCustomChecks(): chkRemoveEmpty.Value = False: chkCollapseBreaks.Value = False: chkNormalizeParaSpacing.Value = False: chkFixIndent.Value = False: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdSelectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkRemoveEmpty.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkCollapseBreaks.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkNormalizeParaSpacing.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        chkFixIndent.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdDeselectAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If fraCustom.Visible Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ClearCustomChecks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Paragraph Fixer"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doRemove As Boolean, doCollapse As Boolean, doNormalize As Boolean, doIndent As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAll.Value Then doRemove = True: doCollapse = True: doNormalize = True: doIndent = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optRemoveEmpty.Value Then doRemove = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optNormalizeSpacing.Value Then doNormalize = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optCustom.Value Then doRemove = chkRemoveEmpty.Value: doCollapse = chkCollapseBreaks.Value: doNormalize = chkNormalizeParaSpacing.Value: doIndent = chkFixIndent.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doRemove Or doCollapse Or doNormalize Or doIndent) Then MsgBox ""No paragraph options selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Preview: highlight empty paragraphs or triple breaks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, cnt As Long: cnt = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If (doRemove Or doCollapse) And Len(Trim$(p.Range.Text)) <= 1 Then p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Paragraph Fixer"", ""Preview complete. "" & cnt & "" items highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Paragraph Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Paragraph Fixer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Remove empty paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntRemove As Long, cntCollapse As Long, cntNormalize As Long, cntIndent As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doRemove Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim i As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For i = ActiveDocument.Paragraphs.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Set p = ActiveDocument.Paragraphs(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Len(Trim$(p.Range.Text)) <= 1 Then p.Range.Delete: cntRemove = cntRemove + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Collapse extra breaks (3+ to 2)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doCollapse Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = ""^p^p^p"": .Replacement.Text = ""^p^p""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Do While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): cntCollapse = cntCollapse + 1: Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Loop While targetRange.Find.Execute(FindText:=""^p^p^p"", Forward:=True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Normalize paragraph spacing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doNormalize Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                With p.Format" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    If .SpaceBefore <> 0 Or .SpaceAfter <> 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                        .SpaceBefore = 0: .SpaceAfter = 0: cntNormalize = cntNormalize + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Fix indent" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doIndent Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If p.LeftIndent <> 0 Then p.LeftIndent = 0: cntIndent = cntIndent + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting targetRange" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doRemove    Then results.Add ""Empty paragraphs removed:        "" & cntRemove" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doCollapse  Then results.Add ""Extra paragraph breaks collapsed: "" & cntCollapse" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doNormalize Then results.Add ""Paragraph spacings normalized:    "" & cntNormalize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doIndent    Then results.Add ""Indents reset to zero:            "" & cntIndent" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Paragraph Cleanup"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmParagraphCleanup_Code = s
End Function

' Duplicate Paragraph Detector
Private Function frmDuplicateDetector_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Duplicate Paragraph Detector" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Finds repeated paragraphs and highlights them, or removes all but the" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  first occurrence.  Three matching methods: exact (case-insensitive)," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  normalized (ignores punctuation and spacing), and fuzzy (groups" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  paragraphs that share a chosen percentage of their words).  Fuzzy" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  matching uses global single-linkage clustering, so the groups it finds" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  do not depend on the order paragraphs appear in the document." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optHighlightOnly.GroupName = ""DupAction""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optRemoveDupes.GroupName = ""DupAction""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optMatchExact.GroupName = ""DupMatch""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optMatchNormalized.GroupName = ""DupMatch""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optMatchFuzzy.GroupName = ""DupMatch""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optFuzzyLoose.GroupName = ""DupThreshold""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optFuzzyMedium.GroupName = ""DupThreshold""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optFuzzyStrict.GroupName = ""DupThreshold""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""DupScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""DupScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optHighlightOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optMatchExact.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optFuzzyMedium.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblFuzzyWarning.Caption = ""Exact and normalized matching are fast.  Fuzzy matching compares every paragraph and is slower on large documents.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optHighlightOnly.Caption = ""Highlight duplicates (preview only)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optRemoveDupes.Caption = ""Remove duplicate paragraphs""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optMatchExact.Caption = ""Exact match""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optMatchNormalized.Caption = ""Normalized match (ignore punctuation and spaces)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optMatchFuzzy.Caption = ""Fuzzy match (word overlap)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optFuzzyLoose.Caption = ""Loose  (50% word overlap)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optFuzzyMedium.Caption = ""Medium  (70% word overlap)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optFuzzyStrict.Caption = ""Strict  (90% word overlap)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub optMatchExact_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblFuzzyWarning.Caption = ""Exact match: paragraphs must be identical apart from letter case.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub optMatchNormalized_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblFuzzyWarning.Caption = ""Normalized match: ignores punctuation, extra spaces, and letter case.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub optMatchFuzzy_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblFuzzyWarning.Caption = ""Fuzzy match: groups paragraphs sharing the chosen percentage of words.  Slower on large documents.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Duplicate Paragraph Detector"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Finds paragraphs that repeat in the document and highlights them, or "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""removes every occurrence except the first.  Blank paragraphs are ignored."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""MATCHING METHOD"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Exact            --  identical apart from letter case."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Ignore punctuation & spacing  --  duplicates even if they differ only"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                       in punctuation, extra spaces, or letter case."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Fuzzy (similar wording)  --  flags paragraphs that share a chosen"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                       percentage of their words; catches near-duplicates "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""                       that were lightly edited.  Slower on large documents."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""FUZZY SENSITIVITY (used only by fuzzy matching)"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Loose (60%)   --  more matches, including loosely similar paragraphs."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Medium (80%)  --  a balanced default."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Strict (90%)  --  fewer matches; only very close paragraphs."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""ACTION"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Highlight only     --  marks duplicates in yellow for review."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove duplicates  --  deletes every occurrence except the first."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to search within the selection only."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Similar paragraphs are grouped together (a paragraph linked to a match, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""which is linked to another, joins the same group), and the first in each"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""group is kept.  Run with Preview first to see what would be affected."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Duplicate Paragraph Detector""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function NormalizeText(ByVal t As String, ByVal stripPunct As Boolean) As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim r As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    r = t" & vbCrLf
