    s = s & "    If Len(r) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Right$(r, 1) = vbCr Then r = Left$(r, Len(r) - 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    r = LCase$(Trim$(r))" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If stripPunct Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim i As Long, ch As String, out As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        out = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For i = 1 To Len(r)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ch = Mid$(r, i, 1)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If (ch >= ""a"" And ch <= ""z"") Or (ch >= ""0"" And ch <= ""9"") Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                out = out & ch" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ElseIf ch = "" "" Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Len(out) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    If Right$(out, 1) <> "" "" Then out = out & "" """ & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        r = Trim$(out)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    NormalizeText = r" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function BuildWordSet(ByVal t As String) As Object" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim d As Object: Set d = CreateObject(""Scripting.Dictionary"")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    d.CompareMode = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim norm As String: norm = NormalizeText(t, True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Len(norm) = 0 Then Set BuildWordSet = d: Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(norm, "" "")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim i As Long, w As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For i = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        w = parts(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Len(w) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Not d.Exists(w) Then d.Add w, 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set BuildWordSet = d" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function JaccardSimilarity(ByVal a As Object, ByVal b As Object) As Double" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If a.Count = 0 And b.Count = 0 Then JaccardSimilarity = 1: Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If a.Count = 0 Or b.Count = 0 Then JaccardSimilarity = 0: Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim shared As Long: shared = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim k As Variant" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If a.Count <= b.Count Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each k In a.Keys" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If b.Exists(k) Then shared = shared + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each k In b.Keys" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If a.Exists(k) Then shared = shared + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    JaccardSimilarity = shared / (a.Count + b.Count - shared)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function FindRoot(ByRef par() As Long, ByVal x As Long) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Do While par(x) <> x" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        par(x) = par(par(x))" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        x = par(x)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    FindRoot = x" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Duplicate Detector"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doRemove As Boolean: doRemove = optRemoveDupes.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim matchMode As Integer: matchMode = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optMatchNormalized.Value Then matchMode = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optMatchFuzzy.Value Then matchMode = 2" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim threshold As Double: threshold = 0.8" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optFuzzyLoose.Value Then threshold = 0.6" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optFuzzyStrict.Value Then threshold = 0.9" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim paras As Collection: Set paras = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Len(Trim$(p.Range.Text)) > 1 Then paras.Add p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If matchMode = 2 And paras.Count > 1500 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If MsgBox(""This scope has "" & paras.Count & "" paragraphs. Fuzzy matching compares them all and may take a while. Continue?"", vbQuestion + vbYesNo, ""Fuzzy Match"") = vbNo Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Duplicate Detector""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Duplicate Detector""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim dupCount As Long: dupCount = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim dupStarts As Collection: Set dupStarts = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim i As Long, j As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If matchMode <= 1 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim dict As Object: Set dict = CreateObject(""Scripting.Dictionary"")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        dict.CompareMode = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim key As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For i = 1 To paras.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Set p = paras(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If matchMode = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                key = NormalizeText(p.Range.Text, False)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                key = NormalizeText(p.Range.Text, True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Len(key) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If dict.Exists(key) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    dupStarts.Add p.Range.Start" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    dupCount = dupCount + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    dict.Add key, i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim n As Long: n = paras.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If n > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim wsArr() As Object" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ReDim wsArr(1 To n)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For i = 1 To n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Set wsArr(i) = BuildWordSet(paras(i).Range.Text)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim par() As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ReDim par(1 To n)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For i = 1 To n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                par(i) = i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim ca As Long, cb As Long, mn As Long, mx As Long, ra As Long, rb As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For i = 1 To n - 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                ca = wsArr(i).Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If ca > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    For j = i + 1 To n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                        cb = wsArr(j).Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                        If cb > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                            If ca <= cb Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                                mn = ca: mx = cb" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                            Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                                mn = cb: mx = ca" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                            If mn / mx >= threshold Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                                If JaccardSimilarity(wsArr(i), wsArr(j)) >= threshold Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                                    ra = FindRoot(par, i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                                    rb = FindRoot(par, j)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                                    If ra <> rb Then par(ra) = rb" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    Next j" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If (i Mod 25 = 0) Then Application.StatusBar = ""Duplicate Detector: comparing "" & i & "" of "" & n & ""...""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim rootMin As Object: Set rootMin = CreateObject(""Scripting.Dictionary"")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim rt As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For i = 1 To n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                rt = CStr(FindRoot(par, i))" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If rootMin.Exists(rt) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    If i < rootMin(rt) Then rootMin(rt) = i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    rootMin.Add rt, i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For i = 1 To n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                rt = CStr(FindRoot(par, i))" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If i <> rootMin(rt) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    paras(i).Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    dupStarts.Add paras(i).Range.Start" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    dupCount = dupCount + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.StatusBar = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Duplicate Detector"", ""Preview complete. "" & dupCount & "" duplicate paragraphs highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doRemove Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ' Remove ONLY paragraphs detected as duplicates, identified by the" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ' start positions captured during detection -- never by highlight" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ' colour. This leaves any pre-existing user highlighting untouched." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim removed As Long: removed = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim dn As Long: dn = dupStarts.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If dn > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim ds() As Long, di As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ReDim ds(1 To dn)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For di = 1 To dn: ds(di) = dupStarts(di): Next di" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ' sort start positions descending so each deletion never shifts" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ' the positions of paragraphs still queued for removal" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim a As Long, b As Long, tmpS As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For a = 1 To dn - 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                For b = a + 1 To dn" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    If ds(b) > ds(a) Then tmpS = ds(a): ds(a) = ds(b): ds(b) = tmpS" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Next b" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next a" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For di = 1 To dn" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                ActiveDocument.Range(ds(di), ds(di)).Paragraphs(1).Range.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                removed = removed + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next di" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Duplicate paragraphs removed: "" & removed" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Duplicate paragraphs highlighted: "" & dupCount" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Select Case matchMode" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Case 0: results.Add ""Match method: exact (case-insensitive)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Case 1: results.Add ""Match method: normalized""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Case 2: results.Add ""Match method: fuzzy ("" & CLng(threshold * 100) & ""%, grouped)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End Select" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Duplicate Detector"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.StatusBar = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmDuplicateDetector_Code = s
End Function

' Font Normalizer
Private Function frmFontNormalizer_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Font Normalizer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Resets direct font overrides back to what the paragraph style defines," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  eliminating the font chaos that accumulates from copy-paste.  Face and" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  size are reset by default; bold, italic, and colour are opt-in since" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  those properties are more likely to be intentional." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFontFace.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFontSize.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkBold.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkItalic.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFontColor.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFontFace.Caption = ""Normalize font face to document default""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFontSize.Caption = ""Normalize font size to style-defined size""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkBold.Caption = ""Normalize direct bold overrides""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkItalic.Caption = ""Normalize direct italic overrides""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFontColor.Caption = ""Remove direct font color overrides""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Font Normalizer"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Resets direct font overrides to match the style defined for each paragraph, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""eliminating the font chaos that accumulates from copying and pasting text"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""between documents, emails, and web pages."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS  (choose which properties to reset)"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Font face   --  Resets the typeface (e.g. Arial, Times New Roman) to the"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                  style's defined font."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Font size   --  Resets the point size to the style's defined size."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Bold        --  Resets bold to the style's setting."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Italic      --  Resets italic to the style's setting."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Font colour --  Resets text colour to the style's defined colour."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""TIP: Start with Face and Size only.  Bold, Italic, and Colour are opt-in "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""because those properties are more likely to be intentional (e.g. a bolded"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""phrase or a deliberately coloured heading within a paragraph)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights paragraphs with direct font overrides."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Font Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Font Normalizer"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doFace As Boolean: doFace = chkFontFace.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doSize As Boolean: doSize = chkFontSize.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doBold As Boolean: doBold = chkBold.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doItalic As Boolean: doItalic = chkItalic.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doColor As Boolean: doColor = chkFontColor.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doFace Or doSize Or doBold Or doItalic Or doColor) Then MsgBox ""No font properties selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Preview: highlight paragraphs with direct font overrides" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph, styleFont As Font, cnt As Long: cnt = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Set styleFont = ActiveDocument.Styles(p.Style).Font" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Not styleFont Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim hasDirect As Boolean: hasDirect = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doFace And p.Range.Font.Name <> styleFont.Name Then hasDirect = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doSize And p.Range.Font.Size <> styleFont.Size And p.Range.Font.Size > 0 Then hasDirect = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If hasDirect Then p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Font Normalizer"", ""Preview complete. "" & cnt & "" paragraphs with direct font overrides highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Font Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Font Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntFixed As Long: cntFixed = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Set styleFont = ActiveDocument.Styles(p.Style).Font" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Not styleFont Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim changed As Boolean: changed = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doFace Then p.Range.Font.Name = styleFont.Name: changed = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doSize Then p.Range.Font.Size = styleFont.Size: changed = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doBold Then p.Range.Font.Bold = styleFont.Bold: changed = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doItalic Then p.Range.Font.Italic = styleFont.Italic: changed = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doColor Then p.Range.Font.Color = styleFont.Color: changed = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If changed Then cntFixed = cntFixed + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting targetRange" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    results.Add ""Paragraphs normalized: "" & cntFixed" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Font Normalizer"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmFontNormalizer_Code = s
End Function

' Table Cleaner
Private Function frmTableCleaner_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Table Cleaner" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Removes empty rows and columns from tables, normalises cell padding" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  to Word defaults (0.04"" top/bottom, 0.08"" left/right), and optionally" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  strips direct table-level formatting, resets or removes border styles, and" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  can convert tables to plain tab-separated text.  Operates on all tables" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  within the target scope." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveEmptyRows.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveEmptyCols.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizePadding.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkStripDirectFormat.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizeBorders.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveBorders.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkConvertToText.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveEmptyRows.Caption = ""Remove empty rows""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveEmptyCols.Caption = ""Remove empty columns""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizePadding.Caption = ""Normalize cell padding""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkStripDirectFormat.Caption = ""Strip direct formatting from cells""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkNormalizeBorders.Caption = ""Normalize border styles""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveBorders.Caption = ""Remove all borders""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkConvertToText.Caption = ""Convert single-column tables to plain text""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Table Cleaner"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Removes structural clutter from tables and normalises their formatting."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Operates on all tables within the target scope."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove empty rows     --  Deletes any row where every cell contains"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                            no text (a lone paragraph mark is not text)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove empty columns  --  Deletes any column where every cell is empty."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Normalise padding     --  Sets all cell padding to Word defaults:"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                            0.04 inch top/bottom,  0.08 inch left/right."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Strip direct format   --  Clears direct formatting overrides from the"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                            table, restoring the table's applied style."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Normalise borders     --  Enables borders on the table using the"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                            current table style."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove all borders    --  Turns off every border on the table."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Convert table to text --  Replaces the table with tab-separated text"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                            (done last; other table options run first)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""TIP: Run 'Remove empty rows/columns' before 'Strip direct format' for "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""the cleanest results.  Use Preview first to see which rows are empty."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to tables in the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Table Cleaner""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function IsRowEmpty(rw As Row) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cel As Cell" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each cel In rw.Cells" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Len(Trim$(cel.Range.Text)) > 2 Then IsRowEmpty = False: Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next cel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    IsRowEmpty = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function IsColEmpty(tbl As Table, colIdx As Long) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim r As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For r = 1 To tbl.Rows.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim cel As Cell: Set cel = tbl.Cell(r, colIdx)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Not cel Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Len(Trim$(cel.Range.Text)) > 2 Then IsColEmpty = False: Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next r" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    IsColEmpty = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Table Cleaner"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doEmptyRows As Boolean: doEmptyRows = chkRemoveEmptyRows.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doEmptyCols As Boolean: doEmptyCols = chkRemoveEmptyCols.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doPadding As Boolean: doPadding = chkNormalizePadding.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doDirectFmt As Boolean: doDirectFmt = chkStripDirectFormat.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doBorders As Boolean: doBorders = chkNormalizeBorders.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doRemoveBorders As Boolean: doRemoveBorders = chkRemoveBorders.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doConvertText As Boolean: doConvertText = chkConvertToText.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doEmptyRows Or doEmptyCols Or doPadding Or doDirectFmt Or doBorders Or doRemoveBorders Or doConvertText) Then MsgBox ""No table options selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Collect tables in scope" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim tbl As Table, tablesInScope As Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set tablesInScope = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each tbl In ActiveDocument.Tables" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If tbl.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And tbl.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then tablesInScope.Add tbl" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next tbl" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If tablesInScope.Count = 0 Then MsgBox ""No tables found in the selected scope."", vbInformation: Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Preview: highlight empty rows and cells" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cnt As Long: cnt = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim t As Variant" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each t In tablesInScope" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Set tbl = t" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doEmptyRows Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim rw As Row" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                For Each rw In tbl.Rows" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    If IsRowEmpty(rw) Then rw.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Next rw" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next t" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Table Cleaner"", ""Preview complete. "" & cnt & "" empty rows highlighted across "" & tablesInScope.Count & "" tables.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Table Cleaner""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Table Cleaner""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntRows As Long, cntCols As Long, cntTables As Long, cntConverted As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim tVar As Variant" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each tVar In tablesInScope" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set tbl = tVar" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doEmptyRows Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim ri As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For ri = tbl.Rows.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If IsRowEmpty(tbl.Rows(ri)) Then tbl.Rows(ri).Delete: cntRows = cntRows + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next ri" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doEmptyCols Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim ci As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For ci = tbl.Columns.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If IsColEmpty(tbl, ci) Then tbl.Columns(ci).Delete: cntCols = cntCols + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next ci" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doPadding Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.TopPadding = InchesToPoints(0.04)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.BottomPadding = InchesToPoints(0.04)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.LeftPadding = InchesToPoints(0.08)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.RightPadding = InchesToPoints(0.08)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doDirectFmt Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.Range.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doBorders Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.Borders.Enable = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doRemoveBorders Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.Borders.Enable = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        cntTables = cntTables + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doConvertText Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            tbl.ConvertToText Separator:=wdSeparateByTabs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            cntConverted = cntConverted + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next tVar" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    results.Add ""Tables processed: "" & cntTables" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doEmptyRows Then results.Add ""Empty rows removed: "" & cntRows" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doEmptyCols Then results.Add ""Empty columns removed: "" & cntCols" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doConvertText Then results.Add ""Tables converted to text: "" & cntConverted" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Table Cleaner"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmTableCleaner_Code = s
End Function

' Break Normalizer
Private Function frmBreakNormalizer_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Break Normalizer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Collapses consecutive section and page breaks -- a common problem in" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  documents assembled from multiple files -- and optionally converts all" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  section breaks to a chosen type: Next Page, Continuous, Even Page," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  or Odd Page." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertNextPage.GroupName = ""BreakMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertContinuous.GroupName = ""BreakMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertEvenPage.GroupName = ""BreakMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertOddPage.GroupName = ""BreakMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""BreakScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""BreakScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkCollapseSectionBreaks.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkCollapsePageBreaks.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkConvertSectionBreaks.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraConvertTo.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertContinuous.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkCollapseSectionBreaks.Caption = ""Collapse consecutive section breaks""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkCollapsePageBreaks.Caption = ""Collapse consecutive page breaks""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkConvertSectionBreaks.Caption = ""Convert section breaks to page breaks""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertNextPage.Caption = ""Next Page""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertContinuous.Caption = ""Continuous""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertEvenPage.Caption = ""Even Page""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optConvertOddPage.Caption = ""Odd Page""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Break Normalizer"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Fixes problems with section and page breaks.  Most common in documents "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""assembled from multiple files, where each source file may have had its"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""own trailing section break."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Collapse section breaks  --  Reduces consecutive section breaks to one."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Collapse page breaks     --  Reduces consecutive manual page breaks to one."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Convert section breaks   --  Changes all section break types in scope to"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                               the type you choose below."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""CONVERSION TYPES"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Next Page   --  Section starts at the top of the next page."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Continuous  --  Section continues on the same page (no forced break)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Even Page   --  Section starts on the next even-numbered page."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Odd Page    --  Section starts on the next odd-numbered page."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: The first section of a document is never converted -- it has no "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""preceding break to change."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights consecutive break sequences in yellow."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Break Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub chkConvertSectionBreaks_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraConvertTo.Enabled = chkConvertSectionBreaks.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Break Normalizer"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doCollSect As Boolean: doCollSect = chkCollapseSectionBreaks.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doCollPage As Boolean: doCollPage = chkCollapsePageBreaks.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doConvert As Boolean: doConvert = chkConvertSectionBreaks.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doCollSect Or doCollPage Or doConvert) Then MsgBox ""No break options selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Determine target section start type for conversion" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetSectStart As Long: targetSectStart = wdSectionContinuous" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optConvertNextPage.Value Then targetSectStart = wdSectionNewPage" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optConvertContinuous.Value Then targetSectStart = wdSectionContinuous" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optConvertEvenPage.Value Then targetSectStart = wdSectionEvenPage" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optConvertOddPage.Value Then targetSectStart = wdSectionOddPage" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Preview: highlight consecutive section/page breaks" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cnt As Long: cnt = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doCollSect Or doCollPage Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doCollSect Then .Text = ""^b^b"": .Replacement.Text = ""^&"": .Replacement.Highlight = True: .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll: cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doCollPage Then .Text = ""^m^m"": .Replacement.Text = ""^&"": .Replacement.Highlight = True: .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll: cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Break Normalizer"", ""Preview complete. Break sequences highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Break Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Break Normalizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntSect As Long, cntPage As Long, cntConv As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doCollSect Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            With targetRange.Find: .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = ""^b^b"": .Replacement.Text = ""^b"": Do While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): cntSect = cntSect + 1: Loop: End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Loop While targetRange.Find.Execute(FindText:=""^b^b"", Forward:=True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doCollPage Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            With targetRange.Find: .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Text = ""^m^m"": .Replacement.Text = ""^m"": Do While .Execute(Replace:=wdReplaceOne): cntPage = cntPage + 1: Loop: End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Loop While targetRange.Find.Execute(FindText:=""^m^m"", Forward:=True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doConvert Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim sec As Section" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each sec In ActiveDocument.Sections" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If sec.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And sec.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If sec.Index > 1 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    sec.PageSetup.SectionStart = targetSectStart" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    cntConv = cntConv + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next sec" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting targetRange" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doCollSect Then results.Add ""Consecutive section breaks collapsed: "" & cntSect" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doCollPage Then results.Add ""Consecutive page breaks collapsed: "" & cntPage" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doConvert Then results.Add ""Section break types converted: "" & cntConv" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Break Normalizer"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmBreakNormalizer_Code = s
End Function

' Document Trim
Private Function frmDocumentTrim_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Document Trim" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Removes trailing empty paragraphs from the end of the document," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  leaving exactly one final paragraph (which Word requires).  Always" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  operates on the full document -- ""end of document"" is inherently" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  document-level, so scope selection is disabled for this tool." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Scope controls disabled -- this tool always operates on the full document" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document (always)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Document Trim"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Removes trailing empty paragraphs from the end of the document, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""leaving exactly one final empty paragraph (which Word requires to exist)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""WHY THIS MATTERS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Documents assembled from multiple files, or saved from templates, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""  often end with many blank paragraphs.  These appear as unwanted blank"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  space at the end of the printed document and can cause unexpected "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""  behaviour with headers, footers, and page numbering in some layouts."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: This tool always operates on the full document.  Scope selection "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""is disabled because 'end of document' is inherently document-level --"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""trimming to the end of a selection would not be meaningful."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights the trailing paragraphs that would be removed."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Run again without Preview to apply."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Document Trim""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Document Trim"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Find trailing empty paragraphs (walk backwards from end)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim totalParas As Long: totalParas = ActiveDocument.Paragraphs.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim trailingCount As Long: trailingCount = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim i As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For i = totalParas To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim p As Paragraph: Set p = ActiveDocument.Paragraphs(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Len(Trim$(p.Range.Text)) <= 1 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            trailingCount = trailingCount + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Exit For" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Always leave at least one paragraph at the end" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim toRemove As Long: toRemove = trailingCount - 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If toRemove <= 0 Then MsgBox ""No trailing empty paragraphs found."", vbInformation: Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ' Highlight the ones that will be removed" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For i = totalParas To totalParas - toRemove + 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ActiveDocument.Paragraphs(i).Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Document Trim"", ""Preview complete. "" & toRemove & "" trailing empty paragraphs highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Document Trim""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Document Trim""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim removed As Long: removed = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim currentTotal As Long: currentTotal = ActiveDocument.Paragraphs.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For i = currentTotal To currentTotal - toRemove + 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ActiveDocument.Paragraphs(i).Range.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        removed = removed + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    results.Add ""Trailing empty paragraphs removed: "" & removed" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Document Trim"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmDocumentTrim_Code = s
End Function

' Direct Formatting Stripper
Private Function frmFormattingStripper_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Direct Formatting Stripper" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Removes manual (direct) character and paragraph formatting while" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  PRESERVING the paragraph style.  Unlike Word built-in Clear Formatting," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  which resets text to the Normal style, this tool returns each paragraph" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  to exactly what its applied style specifies.  Three emphasis modes" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  balance completeness against speed.  Drop caps are preserved by default;" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  untick 'Preserve drop caps' to reset them too." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkResetChar.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkResetPara.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreserveHighlight.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreserveDropCaps.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreserveHighlight.Caption = ""Preserve highlighting""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreserveDropCaps.Caption = ""Preserve drop caps""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optEmphQuick.GroupName = ""StripEmphasis""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optEmphThorough.GroupName = ""StripEmphasis""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optEmphStrip.GroupName = ""StripEmphasis""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optEmphQuick.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblSpeedWarning.Caption = ""Quick mode is fast and preserves emphasis that applies to whole paragraphs.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""StripScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""StripScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkResetChar.Caption = ""Strip direct character formatting""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkResetPara.Caption = ""Strip direct paragraph formatting""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optEmphQuick.Caption = ""Quick  (preserve paragraph-level emphasis)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optEmphThorough.Caption = ""Thorough  (preserve word-level emphasis)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optEmphStrip.Caption = ""Strip  (remove all direct formatting)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub optEmphQuick_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblSpeedWarning.Caption = ""Quick mode is fast and preserves emphasis that applies to whole paragraphs.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub optEmphThorough_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblSpeedWarning.Caption = ""Thorough mode examines every run individually and preserves word-level emphasis.  On a long document this may take a minute or more.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub optEmphStrip_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblSpeedWarning.Caption = ""Strip mode removes all direct formatting, including bold and italic emphasis.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Direct Formatting Stripper"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Removes manual formatting overrides and returns text to what its "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""paragraph style defines.  Unlike Word Clear Formatting (which resets"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""text to the Normal style), this tool keeps your styles intact and strips "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""only the direct formatting layered on top."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""WHAT TO RESET"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Reset character formatting  --  font face, size, colour, spacing."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Reset paragraph formatting  --  indents, line spacing, alignment."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""EMPHASIS HANDLING"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Quick clean      --  Fast.  Preserves bold/italic that applies to a"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                       whole paragraph.  Best for most documents."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Thorough clean   --  Preserves bold/italic on individual words."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                       Slower on large documents (examines every run)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Strip everything --  Removes all emphasis too, for a full reset."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Preserve highlighting keeps text highlight colours through the reset."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Preserve drop caps keeps decorative dropped capitals; untick to reset "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""  them to normal body text as part of the strip."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: Quick mode restores emphasis only when it is uniform across the "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""whole paragraph.  For a paragraph with one bold word among normal text,"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""use Thorough mode to preserve that word precisely."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights paragraphs carrying direct formatting."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Direct Formatting Stripper""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Formatting Stripper"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doResetChar As Boolean: doResetChar = chkResetChar.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doResetPara As Boolean: doResetPara = chkResetPara.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim preserveHL As Boolean: preserveHL = chkPreserveHighlight.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim preserveDC As Boolean: preserveDC = chkPreserveDropCaps.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doResetChar Or doResetPara) Then MsgBox ""Nothing selected to reset. Tick at least one reset option."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim p As Paragraph" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim pcnt As Long: pcnt = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If HasDirectFormatting(p, doResetChar, doResetPara) Then p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: pcnt = pcnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Formatting Stripper"", ""Preview complete. "" & pcnt & "" paragraphs with direct formatting highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim emphMode As Integer: emphMode = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optEmphThorough.Value Then emphMode = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optEmphStrip.Value Then emphMode = 2" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If emphMode = 1 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim totalChars As Long: totalChars = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then totalChars = totalChars + p.Range.Characters.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If totalChars > 20000 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If MsgBox(""This document has "" & totalChars & "" characters in scope."" & vbCrLf & ""Thorough cleaning may take a while. Continue?"", vbQuestion + vbYesNo, ""Thorough Clean"") = vbNo Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Formatting Stripper""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Formatting Stripper""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntReset As Long: cntReset = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim processed As Long: processed = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each p In ActiveDocument.Paragraphs" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And p.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ApplyStripToParagraph p, doResetChar, doResetPara, emphMode, preserveHL, preserveDC" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            cntReset = cntReset + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            processed = processed + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If emphMode = 1 And (processed Mod 25 = 0) Then Application.StatusBar = ""Formatting Stripper: "" & processed & "" paragraphs processed...""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next p" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.StatusBar = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    results.Add ""Paragraphs processed: "" & cntReset" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doResetChar Then results.Add ""Character formatting reset""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doResetPara Then results.Add ""Paragraph formatting reset""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Select Case emphMode" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Case 0: results.Add ""Emphasis: whole-paragraph preserved""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Case 1: results.Add ""Emphasis: word-level preserved""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Case 2: results.Add ""Emphasis: removed""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End Select" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Formatting Stripper"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.StatusBar = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function HasDirectFormatting(p As Paragraph, doChar As Boolean, doPara As Boolean) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    HasDirectFormatting = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim st As Style" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set st = ActiveDocument.Styles(p.Style)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If st Is Nothing Then Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doChar Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Font.Name <> st.Font.Name Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Font.Size <> st.Font.Size And p.Range.Font.Size > 0 Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Font.Bold <> st.Font.Bold Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Font.Italic <> st.Font.Italic Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Range.Font.Color <> st.Font.Color Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doPara And Not HasDirectFormatting Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.LeftIndent <> st.ParagraphFormat.LeftIndent Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.SpaceBefore <> st.ParagraphFormat.SpaceBefore Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.SpaceAfter <> st.ParagraphFormat.SpaceAfter Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If p.Alignment <> st.ParagraphFormat.Alignment Then HasDirectFormatting = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ApplyStripToParagraph(p As Paragraph, doChar As Boolean, doPara As Boolean, emphMode As Integer, preserveHL As Boolean, preserveDropCaps As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not preserveDropCaps Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        p.DropCap.Position = wdDropNone" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not doChar Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doPara Then p.Range.ClearParagraphDirectFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If emphMode = 2 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        p.Range.ClearCharacterDirectFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doPara Then p.Range.ClearParagraphDirectFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ElseIf emphMode = 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim wasBold As Variant, wasItalic As Variant, savedHL As Variant" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        wasBold = p.Range.Font.Bold" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        wasItalic = p.Range.Font.Italic" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        savedHL = p.Range.HighlightColorIndex" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        p.Range.ClearCharacterDirectFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doPara Then p.Range.ClearParagraphDirectFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If wasBold = True Then p.Range.Font.Bold = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If wasItalic = True Then p.Range.Font.Italic = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If preserveHL And savedHL <> wdUndefined And savedHL <> wdNoHighlight Then p.Range.HighlightColorIndex = savedHL" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim n As Long: n = p.Range.Characters.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If n < 1 Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim bArr() As Boolean, iArr() As Boolean, hArr() As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ReDim bArr(1 To n)" & vbCrLf
