    s = s & "        ReDim iArr(1 To n)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ReDim hArr(1 To n)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim ch As Range, idx As Long: idx = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each ch In p.Range.Characters" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            idx = idx + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If idx <= n Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                bArr(idx) = (ch.Font.Bold = True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                iArr(idx) = (ch.Font.Italic = True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                hArr(idx) = ch.HighlightColorIndex" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next ch" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim baseStart As Long: baseStart = p.Range.Start" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        p.Range.ClearCharacterDirectFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doPara Then p.Range.ClearParagraphDirectFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ApplyRuns baseStart, bArr, iArr, hArr, n, preserveHL" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ApplyRuns(baseStart As Long, bArr() As Boolean, iArr() As Boolean, hArr() As Long, n As Long, preserveHL As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim i As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    i = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Do While i <= n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If bArr(i) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim jb As Long: jb = i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Do While jb < n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Not bArr(jb + 1) Then Exit Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                jb = jb + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ActiveDocument.Range(baseStart + i - 1, baseStart + jb).Font.Bold = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            i = jb + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            i = i + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    i = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Do While i <= n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If iArr(i) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim ji As Long: ji = i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Do While ji < n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Not iArr(ji + 1) Then Exit Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                ji = ji + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ActiveDocument.Range(baseStart + i - 1, baseStart + ji).Font.Italic = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            i = ji + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            i = i + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If preserveHL Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        i = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Do While i <= n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If hArr(i) <> wdNoHighlight And hArr(i) <> wdUndefined Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim jh As Long: jh = i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Do While jh < n" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    If hArr(jh + 1) <> hArr(i) Then Exit Do" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    jh = jh + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                ActiveDocument.Range(baseStart + i - 1, baseStart + jh).HighlightColorIndex = hArr(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                i = jh + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                i = i + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Loop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmFormattingStripper_Code = s
End Function

' Master launcher form code (simple)
' Hyperlink Remover
Private Function frmHyperlinkRemover_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Hyperlink Remover" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Removes hyperlinks but keeps the visible text.  Optionally also clears" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  the blue underlined hyperlink formatting, returning the text to its" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  normal style appearance." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveFormat.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""HLScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""HLScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveFormat.Caption = ""Remove hyperlink character style (blue underline)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Hyperlink Remover"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Removes hyperlinks while keeping the visible text.  Pasting from web "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""pages embeds links throughout a document; this strips the links but"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""leaves the words exactly as they are."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Clear hyperlink formatting  --  also removes the blue underlined look"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                                  (resets to the default character font)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                                  Turn off to keep the blue/underline."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights every hyperlink that would be removed."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Hyperlink Remover""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Hyperlink Remover"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doRemoveFormat As Boolean: doRemoveFormat = chkRemoveFormat.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim i As Long, cnt As Long: cnt = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For i = 1 To ActiveDocument.Hyperlinks.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim hp As Hyperlink: Set hp = ActiveDocument.Hyperlinks(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If hp.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And hp.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then hp.Range.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Hyperlink Remover"", ""Preview complete. "" & cnt & "" hyperlinks highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Hyperlink Remover""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Hyperlink Remover""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For i = ActiveDocument.Hyperlinks.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim hl As Hyperlink: Set hl = ActiveDocument.Hyperlinks(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If hl.Range.Start >= targetRange.Start And hl.Range.End <= targetRange.End Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim rs As Long, rEnd As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            rs = hl.Range.Start: rEnd = hl.Range.End" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            hl.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            cnt = cnt + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doRemoveFormat Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Dim rg As Range: Set rg = ActiveDocument.Range(rs, rEnd)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                rg.Style = ActiveDocument.Styles(wdStyleDefaultParagraphFont)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                rg.Font.Underline = wdUnderlineNone" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                rg.Font.ColorIndex = wdAuto" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    results.Add ""Hyperlinks removed: "" & cnt" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doRemoveFormat Then results.Add ""Hyperlink formatting cleared""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Hyperlink Remover"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmHyperlinkRemover_Code = s
End Function

' Soft Return Converter
Private Function frmSoftReturnConverter_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Soft Return Converter" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Converts soft returns (Shift+Enter line breaks) to real paragraph marks," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  or the reverse.  Imported text from PDFs and web pages is often riddled" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  with soft returns that look like paragraph breaks but break styles," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  spell-check, and spacing." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optSoftToPara.GroupName = ""SoftDir""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optParaToSoft.GroupName = ""SoftDir""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optSoftToPara.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""SoftScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""SoftScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optSoftToPara.Caption = ""Convert soft returns (Shift+Enter) to paragraph marks""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optParaToSoft.Caption = ""Convert paragraph marks to soft returns""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Soft Return Converter"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Converts soft returns (line breaks made with Shift+Enter) to real "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""paragraph marks, or the reverse.  Documents imported from PDFs and web"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""pages are often full of soft returns that look like paragraph breaks but "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""are not -- which quietly breaks spell-check, styles, and spacing."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""DIRECTION"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Soft returns to paragraphs  --  the usual fix for imported text."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Paragraphs to soft returns  --  the reverse (less common)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""CAUTION: Converting paragraphs to soft returns merges the affected text "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""into a single paragraph.  Use the reverse only on small selections."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to limit changes to the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights the breaks that would be converted."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Soft Return Converter""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Soft Return Converter"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim softToPara As Boolean: softToPara = optSoftToPara.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim findText As String, replText As String, markChar As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If softToPara Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        findText = ""^l"": replText = ""^p"": markChar = Chr(11)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        findText = ""^p"": replText = ""^l"": markChar = Chr(13)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Text = findText: .Replacement.Text = ""^&"": .Replacement.Highlight = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Forward = True: .Wrap = wdFindStop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim pt As String: pt = targetRange.Text" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim pc As Long: pc = Len(pt) - Len(Replace(pt, markChar, """"))" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Soft Return Converter"", ""Preview complete. "" & pc & "" breaks highlighted.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Soft Return Converter""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Soft Return Converter""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim txt As String: txt = targetRange.Text" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cnt As Long: cnt = Len(txt) - Len(Replace(txt, markChar, """"))" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    With targetRange.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .ClearFormatting: .Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Text = findText: .Replacement.Text = replText" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Forward = True: .Wrap = wdFindStop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If softToPara Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Soft returns converted to paragraphs: "" & cnt" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Paragraphs converted to soft returns: "" & cnt" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Soft Return Converter"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmSoftReturnConverter_Code = s
End Function

' Metadata Scrubber
Private Function frmMetadataScrubber_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Metadata Scrubber" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Strips identifying information (author, company, comment and revision" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  author names, and optionally comments and tracked changes) before a" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  document is shared.  A one-click version of Word Document Inspector." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Always operates on the whole document." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkProperties.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPersonalInfo.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkComments.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRevisions.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document (always)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkProperties.Caption = ""Clear document properties (Title, Author, Company, etc.)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPersonalInfo.Caption = ""Clear personal information (Last Saved By, revision authors)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkComments.Caption = ""Remove all comments""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRevisions.Caption = ""Accept tracked changes and clear revision marks""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Metadata Scrubber"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Strips identifying information from the document before you share it "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""externally -- a one-click version of Word built-in Document Inspector."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Document properties  --  author, company, manager, title, and other"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                           built-in properties."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Personal information --  author names attached to comments and tracked"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                           changes (anonymises them)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Delete all comments  --  removes every comment from the document."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Accept all tracked changes  --  finalises the document and removes the"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                                  revision history."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: This tool always works on the whole document.  Metadata removal "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""may not be reversible with Ctrl+Z, so a copy is saved first if auto-save"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""is on.  Review the result before sharing."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode shows the metadata currently in the document."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Metadata Scrubber""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Metadata Scrubber"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doProps As Boolean: doProps = chkProperties.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doPersonal As Boolean: doPersonal = chkPersonalInfo.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doComments As Boolean: doComments = chkComments.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doRevisions As Boolean: doRevisions = chkRevisions.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doProps Or doPersonal Or doComments Or doRevisions) Then MsgBox ""Nothing selected to remove."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim info As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        info = ""Current document metadata:"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        info = info & ""Author: "" & ActiveDocument.BuiltInDocumentProperties(""Author"").Value & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        info = info & ""Company: "" & ActiveDocument.BuiltInDocumentProperties(""Company"").Value & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        info = info & ""Last author: "" & ActiveDocument.BuiltInDocumentProperties(""Last Author"").Value & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        info = info & ""Comments: "" & ActiveDocument.Comments.Count & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        info = info & ""Tracked changes: "" & ActiveDocument.Revisions.Count & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        info = info & ""Run without Preview to remove the selected items.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Metadata Scrubber"", info" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Metadata Scrubber""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Metadata Scrubber""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doComments Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim cc As Long: cc = ActiveDocument.Comments.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ActiveDocument.DeleteAllComments" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Comments removed: "" & cc" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doRevisions Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim rc As Long: rc = ActiveDocument.Revisions.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ActiveDocument.Revisions.AcceptAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Tracked changes accepted: "" & rc" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doProps Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ActiveDocument.RemoveDocumentInformation wdRDIDocumentProperties" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Document properties cleared""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doPersonal Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ActiveDocument.RemoveDocumentInformation wdRDIRemovePersonalInformation" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Personal information removed""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Metadata Scrubber"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmMetadataScrubber_Code = s
End Function

' Style Cleanup
Private Function frmStyleCleanup_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Style Cleanup" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Removes unused custom styles and optionally remaps common variant styles" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  (Normal (Web), Body Text 2, etc.) to the Normal style.  Built-in Word" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  styles are never deleted.  Always operates on the whole document." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveUnused.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemapVariants.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document (always)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemoveUnused.Caption = ""Remove unused styles""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkRemapVariants.Caption = ""Remap style variants to canonical equivalents""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Style Cleanup"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Removes clutter from the document style list.  Documents assembled from "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""many sources accumulate dozens of unused or duplicate styles."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove unused styles    --  deletes custom styles that no text uses."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                              Built-in Word styles are never deleted."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remap common variants   --  reassigns text in variant styles such as"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                              Normal (Web), Body Text 2, and Body Text 3 "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""                              to the Normal style, then removes them."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: This tool always works on the whole document.  Usage is detected "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""in the main document body; a style used only inside a header, footer, or"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""text box may be treated as unused."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode reports how many unused custom styles were found."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Style Cleanup""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function StyleInUse(st As Style) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim fr As Range: Set fr = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    With fr.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Style = st" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Text = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Forward = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Wrap = wdFindStop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Execute" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        StyleInUse = .Found" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function RemapStyle(fromName As String, toName As String) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim st As Style: Set st = ActiveDocument.Styles(fromName)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If st Is Nothing Then RemapStyle = 0: Exit Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    With ActiveDocument.Content.Find" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .ClearFormatting: .Style = ActiveDocument.Styles(fromName)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Replacement.ClearFormatting: .Replacement.Style = ActiveDocument.Styles(toName)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Text = """": .Replacement.Text = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Forward = True: .Wrap = wdFindStop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        .Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End With" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not st.BuiltIn Then st.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemapStyle = 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Style Cleanup"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doUnused As Boolean: doUnused = chkRemoveUnused.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doRemap As Boolean: doRemap = chkRemapVariants.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doUnused Or doRemap) Then MsgBox ""Nothing selected."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim st As Style" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim preCount As Long: preCount = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each st In ActiveDocument.Styles" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Not st.BuiltIn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Not StyleInUse(st) Then preCount = preCount + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next st" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Style Cleanup"", ""Preview complete. "" & preCount & "" unused custom styles found.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Style Cleanup""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Style Cleanup""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntRemap As Long: cntRemap = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntUnused As Long: cntUnused = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doRemap Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim variants As Variant, vi As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        variants = Array(""Normal (Web)"", ""Body Text 2"", ""Body Text 3"", ""Body Text Indent"", ""Body Text Indent 2"")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For vi = LBound(variants) To UBound(variants)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            cntRemap = cntRemap + RemapStyle(CStr(variants(vi)), ""Normal"")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next vi" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Variant styles remapped to Normal: "" & cntRemap" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doUnused Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim i As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For i = ActiveDocument.Styles.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Set st = ActiveDocument.Styles(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If Not st.BuiltIn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If Not StyleInUse(st) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    st.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    If Err.Number = 0 Then cntUnused = cntUnused + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    Err.Clear" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        results.Add ""Unused custom styles removed: "" & cntUnused" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Style Cleanup"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmStyleCleanup_Code = s
End Function

Private Function frmFootnoteRemover_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Footnote / Endnote Remover" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Deletes footnotes and/or endnotes (reference mark and note text)." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Optionally keeps each note's text inline, in square brackets, at the" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  reference point.  Word renumbers any remaining notes automatically." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Works on the whole document or the current selection." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFootnotes.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkEndnotes.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkKeepTextInline.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFootnotes.Caption = ""Remove footnotes""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkEndnotes.Caption = ""Remove endnotes""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkKeepTextInline.Caption = ""Keep note text inline [in brackets] instead of deleting it""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Caption = ""Selected text only""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Selection.Type = wdSelectionNormal Or Selection.Type = wdSelectionColumn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Footnote / Endnote Remover"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Deletes footnotes and/or endnotes -- both the reference mark in the body "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""and the note text -- to produce a clean text extract."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove footnotes  --  deletes footnotes in the target scope."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove endnotes   --  deletes endnotes in the target scope."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Keep note text inline  --  inserts each note's text at the reference"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                            point in [square brackets] before deleting it."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Word renumbers any remaining notes automatically."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text first to remove only the notes referenced in the selection."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights the reference marks that would be removed."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Footnote / Endnote Remover""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function InScope(r As Range, targetRange As Range) As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    InScope = (r.Start >= targetRange.Start And r.Start <= targetRange.End)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Footnote / Endnote Remover"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim targetRange As Range" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optScopeSelection.Value And optScopeSelection.Enabled Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = GetTargetRange()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set targetRange = ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doFoot As Boolean: doFoot = chkFootnotes.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doEnd As Boolean: doEnd = chkEndnotes.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim keepText As Boolean: keepText = chkKeepTextInline.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doFoot Or doEnd) Then MsgBox ""Select footnotes, endnotes, or both."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim pf As Long, pe As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim fnP As Footnote, enP As Endnote" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doFoot Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For Each fnP In ActiveDocument.Footnotes" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If InScope(fnP.Reference, targetRange) Then fnP.Reference.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: pf = pf + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next fnP" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doEnd Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For Each enP In ActiveDocument.Endnotes" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If InScope(enP.Reference, targetRange) Then enP.Reference.HighlightColorIndex = wdYellow: pe = pe + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next enP" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Footnote / Endnote Remover"", ""Preview complete."" & vbCrLf & ""Footnotes to remove: "" & pf & vbCrLf & ""Endnotes to remove: "" & pe" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Footnote / Endnote Remover""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Footnote / Endnote Remover""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntFoot As Long, cntEnd As Long, i As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doFoot Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For i = ActiveDocument.Footnotes.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim fn As Footnote: Set fn = ActiveDocument.Footnotes(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If InScope(fn.Reference, targetRange) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If keepText Then fn.Reference.InsertAfter "" ["" & Trim$(fn.Range.Text) & ""]""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                fn.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                cntFoot = cntFoot + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doEnd Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For i = ActiveDocument.Endnotes.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Dim en As Endnote: Set en = ActiveDocument.Endnotes(i)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If InScope(en.Reference, targetRange) Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If keepText Then en.Reference.InsertAfter "" ["" & Trim$(en.Range.Text) & ""]""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                en.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                cntEnd = cntEnd + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next i" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doFoot Then results.Add ""Footnotes removed: "" & cntFoot" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doEnd Then results.Add ""Endnotes removed: "" & cntEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If keepText Then results.Add ""Note text kept inline in [brackets]""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Footnote / Endnote Remover"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmFootnoteRemover_Code = s
End Function

Private Function frmHeaderFooterStandardizer_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Header / Footer Standardizer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Standardizes header/footer text across every section and slot" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  (primary, first-page, even-page), or clears all header/footer content." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Standardize can reset the font to the document default, remove paragraph" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  spacing, and set a chosen alignment.  Page numbers and fields are left" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  untouched.  Always operates on the whole document." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optStandardize.GroupName = ""HFMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optClearAll.GroupName = ""HFMode""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAlignLeft.GroupName = ""HFAlign""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAlignCenter.GroupName = ""HFAlign""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAlignRight.GroupName = ""HFAlign""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optStandardize.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHeaders.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFooters.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFont.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkSpacing.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkAlignment.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAlignLeft.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkBreakLinks.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optStandardize.Caption = ""Standardize formatting""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optClearAll.Caption = ""Clear all header/footer content""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHeaders.Caption = ""Include headers""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFooters.Caption = ""Include footers""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFont.Caption = ""Reset font to the document default""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkSpacing.Caption = ""Remove paragraph spacing""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkAlignment.Caption = ""Set alignment""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAlignLeft.Caption = ""Left""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAlignCenter.Caption = ""Center""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optAlignRight.Caption = ""Right""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkBreakLinks.Caption = ""Unlink sections (set each independently)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document (always)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Header / Footer Standardizer"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Makes header and footer formatting consistent across the whole document, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""or clears all header/footer content entirely."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""MODE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Standardize formatting  --  applies the options below to every header "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""and footer area in every section."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Clear all content       --  removes all text from headers/footers."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""STANDARDIZE OPTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Reset font     --  sets header/footer text to the document's default"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                     (Normal style) font and size."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove spacing --  sets paragraph space-before and space-after to zero."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Set alignment  --  left, center, or right."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""AREAS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Include headers / Include footers choose which to process."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Each section has up to three slots: primary, first-page, even-page."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""LINKED SECTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Sections linked to the previous one share its header/footer.  Tick"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  'Unlink sections' to break those links so each is set on its own."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: Page numbers, dates, and other fields are not altered.  Always "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""works on the whole document."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Header / Footer Standardizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ApplyToHF(hf As HeaderFooter, doClear As Boolean, doFont As Boolean, doSpacing As Boolean, doAlign As Boolean, alignVal As Long, baseName As String, baseSize As Single, breakLinks As Boolean, ByRef cntAreas As Long, ByRef cntCleared As Long)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If breakLinks Then hf.LinkToPrevious = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doClear Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Len(Trim$(hf.Range.Text)) > 0 Then cntCleared = cntCleared + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        hf.Range.Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doFont Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            hf.Range.Font.Name = baseName" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            hf.Range.Font.Size = baseSize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doSpacing Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            hf.Range.ParagraphFormat.SpaceBefore = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            hf.Range.ParagraphFormat.SpaceAfter = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doAlign Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            hf.Range.ParagraphFormat.Alignment = alignVal" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cntAreas = cntAreas + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Header / Footer Standardizer"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doClear As Boolean: doClear = optClearAll.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doHeaders As Boolean: doHeaders = chkHeaders.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doFooters As Boolean: doFooters = chkFooters.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doFont As Boolean: doFont = chkFont.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doSpacing As Boolean: doSpacing = chkSpacing.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doAlign As Boolean: doAlign = chkAlignment.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim breakLinks As Boolean: breakLinks = chkBreakLinks.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doHeaders Or doFooters) Then MsgBox ""Select headers, footers, or both."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not doClear And Not (doFont Or doSpacing Or doAlign) Then MsgBox ""Select at least one formatting option to standardize."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim alignVal As Long: alignVal = wdAlignParagraphLeft" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAlignCenter.Value Then alignVal = wdAlignParagraphCenter" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If optAlignRight.Value Then alignVal = wdAlignParagraphRight" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim baseName As String: baseName = ActiveDocument.Styles(wdStyleNormal).Font.Name" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim baseSize As Single: baseSize = ActiveDocument.Styles(wdStyleNormal).Font.Size" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim sec As Section, hf As HeaderFooter" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim pc As Long: pc = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For Each sec In ActiveDocument.Sections" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doHeaders Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                For Each hf In sec.Headers" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    pc = pc + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Next hf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doFooters Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                For Each hf In sec.Footers" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                    pc = pc + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                Next hf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next sec" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim verb As String: verb = ""standardized""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doClear Then verb = ""cleared""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Header / Footer Standardizer"", ""Preview complete."" & vbCrLf & pc & "" header/footer areas across "" & ActiveDocument.Sections.Count & "" sections would be "" & verb & "".""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Header / Footer Standardizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Header / Footer Standardizer""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim cntAreas As Long, cntCleared As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For Each sec In ActiveDocument.Sections" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doHeaders Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For Each hf In sec.Headers" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                ApplyToHF hf, doClear, doFont, doSpacing, doAlign, alignVal, baseName, baseSize, breakLinks, cntAreas, cntCleared" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next hf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doFooters Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            For Each hf In sec.Footers" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                ApplyToHF hf, doClear, doFont, doSpacing, doAlign, alignVal, baseName, baseSize, breakLinks, cntAreas, cntCleared" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Next hf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next sec" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    results.Add ""Header/footer areas processed: "" & cntAreas" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doClear Then results.Add ""Areas that had content cleared: "" & cntCleared" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Header / Footer Standardizer"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmHeaderFooterStandardizer_Code = s
End Function

Private Function frmObjectRemover_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then ReturnToMainAfterToolClose" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Remove Objects & Elements" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  Deletes embedded objects/elements that arrive as clutter: pictures," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  text boxes, frames (text kept), horizontal lines, HTML/ActiveX controls," & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  hidden text, and (with confirmation) whole tables.  Every option is OFF" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  by default.  Operates on the main document body only -- header/footer" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "'  objects are left untouched.  Some deletions may not be fully reversible." & vbCrLf
    s = s & "' --------------------------------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPictures.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkTextBoxes.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFrames.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHorizontalLines.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHtmlControls.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHiddenText.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkTables.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPictures.Caption = ""Pictures (inline and floating images)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkTextBoxes.Caption = ""Text boxes (and their contents)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkFrames.Caption = ""Frames (remove frame, keep the text)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHorizontalLines.Caption = ""Horizontal lines""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHtmlControls.Caption = ""HTML / ActiveX form controls""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkHiddenText.Caption = ""Hidden text""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkTables.Caption = ""Tables -- DELETES tables and all their contents""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.GroupName = ""ObjScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.GroupName = ""ObjScope""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeDocument.Caption = ""Entire document (always)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    optScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    fraScopeSelection.Enabled = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Caption = ""Preview only (highlight, do not change)""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutCleanupToolForm Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelp_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Remove Objects & Elements"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Deletes embedded objects and elements that often arrive as clutter from "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""web pages, PDFs, or email -- so you can reduce a document to clean text."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""WHAT EACH OPTION REMOVES"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Pictures         --  inline and floating images."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Text boxes       --  floating text boxes AND the text inside them."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Frames           --  the frame container; the text inside is kept."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Horizontal lines --  the inserted horizontal-line graphic."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  HTML/ActiveX     --  form controls (buttons, input boxes) from web paste."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Hidden text      --  text formatted as hidden."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Tables           --  DELETES whole tables and all their contents."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""IMPORTANT CAUTIONS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  - Every option is OFF by default; tick only what you want removed."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  - Removing tables is destructive and is NOT the same as Table Cleaner's"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""    'Convert table to text', which keeps the content.  You will be asked"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""    to confirm before any tables are deleted."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  - Deleting pictures and shapes may not always be fully reversible by"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""    Undo, depending on your Word version.  Work on a copy if unsure."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  - Works on the main document body only; objects in headers and footers"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""    (such as logos) are left untouched."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode reports a count of what would be removed (it cannot "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""highlight an object that is about to be deleted)."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Remove Objects & Elements""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ProcessPictures(doDelete As Boolean) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim c As Long, k As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For k = ActiveDocument.InlineShapes.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Select Case ActiveDocument.InlineShapes(k).Type" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Case wdInlineShapePicture, wdInlineShapeLinkedPicture" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                c = c + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doDelete Then ActiveDocument.InlineShapes(k).Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End Select" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For k = ActiveDocument.Shapes.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Select Case ActiveDocument.Shapes(k).Type" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Case msoPicture, msoLinkedPicture" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                c = c + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                If doDelete Then ActiveDocument.Shapes(k).Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End Select" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ProcessPictures = c" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ProcessTextBoxes(doDelete As Boolean) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim c As Long, k As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For k = ActiveDocument.Shapes.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If ActiveDocument.Shapes(k).Type = msoTextBox Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            c = c + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doDelete Then ActiveDocument.Shapes(k).Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ProcessTextBoxes = c" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ProcessFrames(doDelete As Boolean) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim c As Long, k As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    c = ActiveDocument.Frames.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doDelete Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For k = ActiveDocument.Frames.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ActiveDocument.Frames(k).Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ProcessFrames = c" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ProcessHorizontalLines(doDelete As Boolean) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim c As Long, k As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For k = ActiveDocument.InlineShapes.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If ActiveDocument.InlineShapes(k).Type = wdInlineShapeHorizontalLine Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            c = c + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doDelete Then ActiveDocument.InlineShapes(k).Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ProcessHorizontalLines = c" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ProcessHtmlControls(doDelete As Boolean) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim c As Long, k As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    For k = ActiveDocument.InlineShapes.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If ActiveDocument.InlineShapes(k).Type = wdInlineShapeOLEControlObject Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            c = c + 1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If doDelete Then ActiveDocument.InlineShapes(k).Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ProcessHtmlControls = c" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ProcessTables(doDelete As Boolean) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim c As Long, k As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    c = ActiveDocument.Tables.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doDelete Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        For k = ActiveDocument.Tables.Count To 1 Step -1" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ActiveDocument.Tables(k).Delete" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Next k" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ProcessTables = c" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Function ProcessHiddenText(doDelete As Boolean) As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim rng As Range: Set rng = ActiveDocument.Content.Duplicate" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    rng.Find.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    rng.Find.Text = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    rng.Find.Font.Hidden = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    rng.Find.Forward = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    rng.Find.Wrap = wdFindStop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim found As Boolean: found = rng.Find.Execute" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If found And doDelete Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim r2 As Range: Set r2 = ActiveDocument.Content.Duplicate" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        r2.Find.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        r2.Find.Text = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        r2.Find.Font.Hidden = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        r2.Find.Replacement.ClearFormatting" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        r2.Find.Replacement.Text = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        r2.Find.Execute Replace:=wdReplaceAll" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If found Then ProcessHiddenText = 1 Else ProcessHiddenText = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Function" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    PreviewFromPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub PreviewFromPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdReset_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    UserForm_Initialize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub RunAfterPreview()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkPreviewOnly.Value = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdRun_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdRun_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If ActiveDocument.ProtectionType <> wdNoProtection Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document is protected. Please remove protection before running cleanup."", vbExclamation, ""Document Protected"": Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not GuardBeforeCleanup(""Remove Objects"") Then Unload Me: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim previewOnly As Boolean: previewOnly = chkPreviewOnly.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doPic As Boolean: doPic = chkPictures.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doTxt As Boolean: doTxt = chkTextBoxes.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doFra As Boolean: doFra = chkFrames.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doHL As Boolean: doHL = chkHorizontalLines.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doHtml As Boolean: doHtml = chkHtmlControls.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doHid As Boolean: doHid = chkHiddenText.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim doTab As Boolean: doTab = chkTables.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not (doPic Or doTxt Or doFra Or doHL Or doHtml Or doHid Or doTab) Then MsgBox ""Select at least one element type to remove."", vbInformation: Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If previewOnly Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim msg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        msg = ""Preview -- elements that would be removed (entire document):""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doPic Then msg = msg & vbCrLf & ""Pictures: "" & ProcessPictures(False)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doTxt Then msg = msg & vbCrLf & ""Text boxes: "" & ProcessTextBoxes(False)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doFra Then msg = msg & vbCrLf & ""Frames: "" & ProcessFrames(False)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doHL Then msg = msg & vbCrLf & ""Horizontal lines: "" & ProcessHorizontalLines(False)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doHtml Then msg = msg & vbCrLf & ""HTML/ActiveX controls: "" & ProcessHtmlControls(False)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doTab Then msg = msg & vbCrLf & ""Tables: "" & ProcessTables(False)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If doHid Then msg = msg & vbCrLf & ""Hidden text present: "" & IIf(ProcessHiddenText(False) > 0, ""Yes"", ""No"")" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowPreviewActions Me, ""Object Remover"", msg" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doTab Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim tcount As Long: tcount = ActiveDocument.Tables.Count" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If tcount > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            If MsgBox(""This will permanently DELETE "" & tcount & "" table(s) and ALL their contents."" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & ""(Different from Table Cleaner's 'Convert table to text', which keeps the content.)"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & ""Continue?"", vbExclamation + vbYesNo, ""Delete Tables"") = vbNo Then Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupStart ""Remove Objects""" & vbCrLf
