    s = s & "    Dim undoRec As UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set undoRec = Application.UndoRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.StartCustomRecord ""Cleanup Suite - Remove Objects""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo RunErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim results As Collection: Set results = New Collection" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doPic Then results.Add ""Pictures removed: "" & ProcessPictures(True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doTxt Then results.Add ""Text boxes removed: "" & ProcessTextBoxes(True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doFra Then results.Add ""Frames removed (text kept): "" & ProcessFrames(True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doHL Then results.Add ""Horizontal lines removed: "" & ProcessHorizontalLines(True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doHtml Then results.Add ""HTML/ActiveX controls removed: "" & ProcessHtmlControls(True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doTab Then results.Add ""Tables removed: "" & ProcessTables(True)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If doHid Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If ProcessHiddenText(True) > 0 Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            results.Add ""Hidden text removed""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            results.Add ""No hidden text found""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ShowCleanupReport ""Remove Objects"", results" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    undoRec.EndCustomRecord" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "RunErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrNumber As Long" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim originalErrDescription As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrNumber = Err.Number" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    originalErrDescription = Err.Description" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Application.ScreenUpdating = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next: undoRec.EndCustomRecord: On Error GoTo 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MarkCleanupEnd" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim errMsg As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = ""An unexpected error occurred: "" & originalErrNumber & "" - "" & originalErrDescription" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    errMsg = errMsg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Some changes may have been made to the document."" & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "             ""Use Word's Undo command (Ctrl+Z) after closing this message if you want to roll them back.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox errMsg, vbCritical, ""Cleanup Error""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmObjectRemover_Code = s
End Function

Private Function frmPreviewActions_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private mSourceForm As Object" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private mToolName As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private mPreviewOn As Boolean" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private mSummaryText As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Public Sub Configure(sourceForm As Object, toolName As String, summaryText As String)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set mSourceForm = sourceForm" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    mToolName = toolName" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    mSummaryText = summaryText" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    mPreviewOn = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Caption = ""Preview Actions""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblTitle.Caption = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblHint.Caption = toolName" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblSummary.Caption = mSummaryText" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdApply.Caption = ""Apply""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdPreview.Caption = ""Preview is ON""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdPreview.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdPreview.Visible = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdClear.Caption = ""Reconfigure""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    LayoutPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub LayoutPanel()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Const FORM_W As Single = 300" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Const FORM_H As Single = 90" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Const CONTENT_W As Single = 262" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Const M As Single = 8" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Const BH As Single = 18" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Const GAP As Single = 5" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Const BTN_W As Single = 84" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Width = FORM_W" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Height = FORM_H" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblTitle.Caption = """"" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblTitle.Visible = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    StyleActionBar" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblTitle.Move 0, 0, 0, 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblHint.Move M, 2, CONTENT_W, 12" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdPreview.Move M, 17, BTN_W, BH" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdClear.Move M + BTN_W + GAP, 17, BTN_W, BH" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    cmdApply.Move M + (2 * (BTN_W + GAP)), 17, BTN_W, BH" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblSummary.Move M, 40, CONTENT_W, 16" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblTitle.Visible = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblTitle.WordWrap = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblSummary.WordWrap = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblHint.WordWrap = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    lblHint.TextAlign = 2" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub StyleActionBar()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.BackColor = RGB(248, 249, 251)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.SpecialEffect = 0" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    StyleLabel lblTitle, 11, False, RGB(0, 0, 0)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    StyleLabel lblHint, 8, True, RGB(0, 0, 0)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    StyleLabel lblSummary, 7.5, False, RGB(0, 0, 0)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    StyleButton cmdApply, True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    StyleButton cmdPreview, True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    StyleButton cmdClear, True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub StyleLabel(ctl As Object, fontSize As Single, isBold As Boolean, fontColor As Long)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.Font.Name = ""Segoe UI""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.Font.Size = fontSize" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.Font.Bold = isBold" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.ForeColor = fontColor" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.BackColor = RGB(248, 249, 251)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.AutoSize = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub StyleButton(ctl As Object, isBold As Boolean)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error Resume Next" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.Font.Name = ""Segoe UI""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.Font.Size = 7.5" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.Font.Bold = isBold" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.BackColor = RGB(255, 255, 255)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ctl.ForeColor = RGB(0, 0, 0)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub ShowModelessAtCurrentPosition()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim panelLeft As Single" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim panelTop As Single" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    panelLeft = Me.Left" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    panelTop = Me.Top" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Hide" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Show vbModeless" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Left = panelLeft" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Top = panelTop" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdApply_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo ApplyErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not DocumentHasVisibleContent() Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        RemoveAllHighlighting ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        MsgBox ""This document appears to be blank. There is nothing to apply."", vbInformation, ""Preview Actions""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Me.Hide" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Not mSourceForm Is Nothing Then mSourceForm.Hide" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set gPreviewActionPanel = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If GetReturnToMainAfterApplySetting() Then ShowCleanupSuiteLauncher" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Hide" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not mSourceForm Is Nothing Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        CallByName mSourceForm, ""RunAfterPreview"", VbMethod" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set gPreviewActionPanel = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If GetReturnToMainAfterApplySetting() Then ShowCleanupSuiteLauncher" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "ApplyErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox ""Could not apply the preview: "" & Err.Description, vbExclamation, ""Preview Actions""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPreview_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    On Error GoTo PreviewErr" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If mPreviewOn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        RemoveAllHighlighting ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        mPreviewOn = False" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        cmdPreview.Caption = ""Preview is OFF""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        cmdPreview.Enabled = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        cmdPreview.Visible = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        lblSummary.Caption = mSummaryText" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        LayoutPanel" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        ShowModelessAtCurrentPosition" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Dim src As Object" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set src = mSourceForm" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        RememberPreviewActionPanelPosition Me.Left, Me.Top" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set gPreviewActionPanel = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Me.Hide" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If Not src Is Nothing Then CallByName src, ""PreviewFromPanel"", VbMethod" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Exit Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "PreviewErr:" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox ""Could not refresh the preview: "" & Err.Description, vbExclamation, ""Preview Actions""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdClear_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    RemoveAllHighlighting ActiveDocument.Content" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Hide" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If Not mSourceForm Is Nothing Then mSourceForm.Show" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Set gPreviewActionPanel = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Unload Me" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    If CloseMode = vbFormControlMenu Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Cancel = True" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        If mPreviewOn Then" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            cmdClear_Click" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Set gPreviewActionPanel = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            Me.Hide" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Else" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        Set gPreviewActionPanel = Nothing" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & ""
    frmPreviewActions_Code = s
End Function

Private Function frmCleanupSuiteLauncher_Code() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "Option Explicit" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Me.Caption = ""Cleanup Suite""" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "    lblLauncherSubtitle.TextAlign = 2" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkReturnToMainAfterApply.Caption = ""Return to main menu after apply""" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "        Dim msg As String" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "              ""Without it, if something goes wrong during cleanup, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "              ""Word's recovery file may not have a clean pre-run version to fall back to."" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "            chkAutoSave.Value = True" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "        End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    End If" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    SetAutoSaveSetting chkAutoSave.Value" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdResetAll_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    ResetCleanupSuiteDefaults" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkReturnToMainAfterApply.Value = GetReturnToMainAfterApplySetting()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkReturnToMainAfterClose.Value = GetReturnToMainAfterCloseSetting()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    chkAutoSave.Value = GetAutoSaveSetting()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox ""All Cleanup Suite tool settings have been reset to defaults."", vbInformation, ""Cleanup Suite""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdUnicode_Click(): Me.Hide: frmUnicodeCleanup.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdPunctuation_Click(): Me.Hide: frmPunctuationCleanup.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdSpacing_Click(): Me.Hide: frmSpacingCleanup.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdCapitalization_Click(): Me.Hide: frmCapitalizationCleanup.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "Private Sub cmdSoftReturn_Click(): Me.Hide: frmSoftReturnConverter.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdMetadata_Click(): Me.Hide: frmMetadataScrubber.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdStyleClean_Click(): Me.Hide: frmStyleCleanup.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdFootnote_Click(): Me.Hide: frmFootnoteRemover.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHeaderFooter_Click(): Me.Hide: frmHeaderFooterStandardizer.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdObjectRemover_Click(): Me.Hide: frmObjectRemover.Show: End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelpUnicode_Click()" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Invisible Unicode Cleaner"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Removes Unicode characters that are invisible on screen but cause problems "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""for search, sorting, comparison, and automated text processing."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""These characters accumulate from web copy-paste, PDF export, and "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""cross-platform file exchange."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""CHARACTER TYPES"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  NBSP  (U+00A0)  Non-Breaking Space: a space that prevents line breaks."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  ZWSP  (U+200B)  Zero-Width Space: completely invisible; affects word break."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  ZWNJ  (U+200C)  Zero-Width Non-Joiner: controls ligature/joining behaviour."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  ZWJ   (U+200D)  Zero-Width Joiner: forces joined rendering."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  BOM   (U+FEFF)  Byte Order Mark: encoding marker; never belongs in text."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  SHY   (U+00AD)  Soft Hyphen: invisible optional hyphen."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "    Dim h As String" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = ""Duplicate Paragraph Detector"" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "            ""removes every occurrence except the first.  Blank paragraphs are ignored."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""MATCHING METHOD"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Exact            --  identical apart from letter case."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Ignore punctuation & spacing  --  duplicates even if they differ only"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                       in punctuation, extra spaces, or letter case."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Fuzzy (similar wording)  --  flags paragraphs that share a chosen"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""                       percentage of their words; catches near-duplicates "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""                       that were lightly edited.  Slower on large documents."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""FUZZY SENSITIVITY (used only by fuzzy matching)"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Loose (60%)   --  more matches, including loosely similar paragraphs."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Medium (80%)  --  a balanced default."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Strict (90%)  --  fewer matches; only very close paragraphs."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Highlight only     --  marks duplicates in yellow for review."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Remove duplicates  --  deletes every occurrence except the first."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""SCOPE"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Select text before opening to search within the selection only."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Duplicate Paragraph Detector""" & vbCrLf

    s = s & "    h = h & ""WHY THIS MATTERS"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  Documents assembled from multiple files, or saved from templates, "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""  often end with many blank paragraphs.  These appear as unwanted blank"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""  space at the end of the printed document and can cause unexpected "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""  behaviour with headers, footers, and page numbering in some layouts."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""NOTE: This tool always operates on the full document.  Scope selection "" & _" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "            ""is disabled because 'end of document' is inherently document-level --"" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""trimming to the end of a selection would not be meaningful."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Preview mode highlights the trailing paragraphs that would be removed."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    h = h & ""Run again without Preview to apply."" & vbCrLf" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    MsgBox h, vbInformation, ""Help  --  Document Trim""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "End Sub" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "Private Sub cmdHelpFormat_Click()" & vbCrLf

' ===========================================================================
' MODULE: modRibbonSetup
' PURPOSE: Writes the CustomUI ribbon XML into the target .dotm / .docm file
'          so that a single "Cleanup Suite" button appears on the Add-ins tab.
'
' HOW TO USE
' ----------
' 1. Run InstallCleanupSuite (above) first so all VBA modules exist.
' 2. Save the document as a Macro-Enabled Template (.dotm) or
'    Macro-Enabled Document (.docm) -- ordinary .docx files cannot hold
'    ribbon XML.
' 3. CLOSE Word completely (the file must not be open while we edit the ZIP).
' 4. Run InjectRibbonXml below from a SEPARATE helper document, passing
'    the full path to your saved .dotm / .docm.
'    e.g.  InjectRibbonXml "C:\Users\You\Documents\CleanupSuite.dotm"
' 5. Re-open the file.  The "Cleanup Suite" button will appear on the
'    Add-ins tab.  Click it once -- Word may ask you to trust the macros.
'
' WHAT THE BUTTON DOES
' --------------------
' It calls ShowCleanupSuiteLauncher, which is already installed by the
' main installer.  The ribbon just gives you a one-click entry point.
'
' RIBBON PLACEMENT
' ----------------
' The XML below places the button in a new group "Cleanup Suite" on the
' built-in Add-ins tab (idMso="TabAddIns").  If you prefer a different
' tab, change the insertAfterMso attribute or wrap in a <tab> element.
' ===========================================================================

' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
' The ribbon XML that will be injected into /customUI/customUI14.xml.
' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function RIBBON_XML() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & "<?xml version=""1.0"" encoding=""UTF-8""?>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "<mso:customUI xmlns:mso=""http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "              onLoad=""RibbonOnLoad"">" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "  <mso:ribbon>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    <mso:tabs>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "      <mso:tab idMso=""TabAddIns"">" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        <mso:group id=""grpCleanupSuite""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                   label=""Cleanup Suite""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                   insertAfterMso=""GroupAddInsMenuCommands"">" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "          <mso:button id=""btnCleanupLauncher""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                      label=""Cleanup Suite""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                      screentip=""Open the Professional Cleanup Suite""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                      supertip=""Launches the master cleanup launcher with all of the suite cleanup tools.""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                      imageMso=""ReviewNewComment""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                      onAction=""ShowCleanupSuiteLauncher""" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "                      size=""large""/>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "        </mso:group>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "      </mso:tab>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "    </mso:tabs>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "  </mso:ribbon>" & vbCrLf
    s = s & "</mso:customUI>"
    RIBBON_XML = s
End Function

' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Ribbon load callback is installed into modCleanupLauncher by the main
' installer above.  It is NOT declared here to avoid an "Ambiguous name
' detected" compile error caused by having two public RibbonOnLoad subs
' in the same VBA project.
' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
' InjectRibbonXml -- call this ONCE, with the file CLOSED in Word.
'
'   filePath   Full path to the .dotm or .docm file.
'
' The routine:
'   1. Opens the file as a ZIP (Office Open XML is a ZIP archive).
'   2. Adds / replaces the /customUI/customUI14.xml part.
'   3. Adds / updates the [Content_Types].xml entry for the new part.
'   4. Adds / updates the _rels\.rels relationship entry.
'   5. Saves and closes the ZIP.
' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub InjectRibbonXml(filePath As String)
    If Len(filePath) = 0 Then
        MsgBox "Please supply the full path to your .dotm or .docm file.", _
               vbExclamation, "InjectRibbonXml"
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim ext As String: ext = LCase$(Right$(filePath, 5))
    If ext <> ".dotm" And ext <> ".docm" Then
        MsgBox "The file must be a .dotm or .docm (macro-enabled format)." & vbCrLf & _
               "Ordinary .docx files cannot contain ribbon XML.", _
               vbExclamation, "InjectRibbonXml"
        Exit Sub
    End If

    If Dir(filePath) = "" Then
        MsgBox "File not found:" & vbCrLf & filePath, vbCritical, "InjectRibbonXml"
        Exit Sub
    End If

    On Error GoTo RibbonErr

    Dim fso As Object
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")

    Dim tempRoot As String
    tempRoot = UniqueTempFolder(fso, "CleanupSuiteRibbon")

    Dim bakPath As String
    bakPath = filePath & ".ribbon_backup"

    Dim zipPath As String
    zipPath = filePath & ".ribbon_work.zip"

    If fso.FileExists(bakPath) Then fso.DeleteFile bakPath, True
    If fso.FileExists(zipPath) Then fso.DeleteFile zipPath, True
    EnsureFolder fso, tempRoot
    EnsureFolder fso, tempRoot & "\customUI"

    FileCopy filePath, bakPath
    FileCopy filePath, zipPath

    Dim oShell As Object
    Set oShell = CreateObject("Shell.Application")

    Dim zipNs As Object
    Set zipNs = oShell.NameSpace(zipPath)
    If zipNs Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3101, "InjectRibbonXml", "Could not open the Office file as a ZIP package."

    oShell.NameSpace(tempRoot).CopyHere zipNs.Items, 16
    WaitForShellCopy tempRoot & "\[Content_Types].xml", 15000

    WriteTextFileUtf8 tempRoot & "\customUI\customUI14.xml", RIBBON_XML

    Dim contentTypesPath As String
    contentTypesPath = tempRoot & "\[Content_Types].xml"
    Dim contentTypesXml As String
    contentTypesXml = ReadTextFileUtf8(contentTypesPath)
    contentTypesXml = RemoveXmlElementByAttribute(contentTypesXml, "Override", "PartName", "/customUI/customUI14.xml")
    contentTypesXml = InsertBeforeClosingTag(contentTypesXml, "Types", _
        "  <Override PartName=""/customUI/customUI14.xml"" ContentType=""application/xml""/>" & vbCrLf)
    WriteTextFileUtf8 contentTypesPath, contentTypesXml

    Dim relsPath As String
    relsPath = tempRoot & "\_rels\.rels"
    Dim relsXml As String
    relsXml = ReadTextFileUtf8(relsPath)
    relsXml = RemoveXmlElementByAttribute(relsXml, "Relationship", "Target", "customUI/customUI14.xml")
    relsXml = RemoveXmlElementByAttribute(relsXml, "Relationship", "Target", "/customUI/customUI14.xml")
    relsXml = InsertBeforeClosingTag(relsXml, "Relationships", _
        "  <Relationship Id=""" & NextRelationshipId(relsXml) & """ Type=""http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2007/relationships/ui/extensibility"" Target=""customUI/customUI14.xml""/>" & vbCrLf)
    WriteTextFileUtf8 relsPath, relsXml

    fso.DeleteFile zipPath, True
    CreateEmptyZip zipPath
    Set zipNs = oShell.NameSpace(zipPath)
    If zipNs Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3102, "InjectRibbonXml", "Could not create the replacement ZIP package."

    zipNs.CopyHere fso.GetFolder(tempRoot).Items, 16
    WaitForZipItems zipNs, fso.GetFolder(tempRoot).Files.Count + fso.GetFolder(tempRoot).SubFolders.Count, 20000
    Wait 3000

    fso.DeleteFile filePath, True
    fso.MoveFile zipPath, filePath
    fso.DeleteFolder tempRoot, True

    MsgBox "Ribbon XML was installed successfully." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "A backup of the original file was saved as:" & vbCrLf & bakPath & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Re-open the file in Word and use the Cleanup Suite button on the Add-ins tab.", _
           vbInformation, "InjectRibbonXml"
    Exit Sub

RibbonErr:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not fso Is Nothing Then
        If Len(zipPath) > 0 And fso.FileExists(zipPath) Then fso.DeleteFile zipPath, True
        If Len(tempRoot) > 0 And fso.FolderExists(tempRoot) Then fso.DeleteFolder tempRoot, True
        If Len(bakPath) > 0 And fso.FileExists(bakPath) And fso.FileExists(filePath) = False Then
            fso.CopyFile bakPath, filePath, True
        End If
    End If
    MsgBox "Ribbon injection failed." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Error " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.Description & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Your original file was left unchanged or restored from backup.", _
           vbCritical, "InjectRibbonXml"
End Sub

' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
' Helpers for ZIP rebuilding and package XML patching.
' ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub Wait(ms As Long)
    Dim t As Single: t = Timer
    Do While Timer < t + ms / 1000
        DoEvents
    Loop
End Sub

Private Function UniqueTempFolder(fso As Object, prefix As String) As String
    Dim basePath As String
    basePath = Environ$("TEMP")
    If Len(basePath) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3106, "InjectRibbonXml", "TEMP environment variable is not set."

    Dim i As Long
    Dim candidate As String
    For i = 1 To 100
        candidate = basePath & "\" & prefix & "_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(i)
        If Not fso.FolderExists(candidate) And Not fso.FileExists(candidate) Then
            UniqueTempFolder = candidate
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i

    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3107, "InjectRibbonXml", "Could not allocate a unique temporary folder."
End Function

Private Sub EnsureFolder(fso As Object, folderPath As String)
    If fso.FolderExists(folderPath) Then Exit Sub

    Dim parentPath As String
    parentPath = fso.GetParentFolderName(folderPath)
    If Len(parentPath) > 0 And Not fso.FolderExists(parentPath) Then
        EnsureFolder fso, parentPath
    End If

    On Error GoTo FolderErr
    fso.CreateFolder folderPath
    Exit Sub

FolderErr:
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3108, "InjectRibbonXml", _
        "Could not create temporary folder:" & vbCrLf & folderPath & vbCrLf & _
        "Error " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.Description
End Sub

Private Sub WaitForShellCopy(requiredPath As String, timeoutMs As Long)
    Dim waited As Long
    Do While Dir(requiredPath) = "" And waited < timeoutMs
        Wait 200
        waited = waited + 200
    Loop
    If Dir(requiredPath) = "" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3103, "InjectRibbonXml", "Timed out while unpacking the Office file."
End Sub

Private Sub WaitForZipItems(zipNs As Object, expectedTopLevelItems As Long, timeoutMs As Long)
    Dim waited As Long
    Do While zipNs.Items.Count < expectedTopLevelItems And waited < timeoutMs
        Wait 250
        waited = waited + 250
    Loop
    If zipNs.Items.Count < expectedTopLevelItems Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3104, "InjectRibbonXml", "Timed out while rebuilding the Office file."
End Sub

Private Sub CreateEmptyZip(zipPath As String)
    Dim emptyZip(0 To 21) As Byte
    emptyZip(0) = 80
    emptyZip(1) = 75
    emptyZip(2) = 5
    emptyZip(3) = 6

    Dim ff As Integer
    ff = FreeFile
    Open zipPath For Binary As #ff
    Put #ff, , emptyZip
    Close #ff
End Sub

Private Function ReadTextFileUtf8(filePath As String) As String
    Dim stm As Object
    Set stm = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stm.Type = 2
    stm.Charset = "utf-8"
    stm.Open
    stm.LoadFromFile filePath
    ReadTextFileUtf8 = stm.ReadText
    stm.Close
End Function

Private Sub WriteTextFileUtf8(filePath As String, text As String)
    Dim stm As Object
    Set stm = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stm.Type = 2
    stm.Charset = "utf-8"
    stm.Open
    stm.WriteText text
    stm.SaveToFile filePath, 2
    stm.Close
End Sub

Private Function InsertBeforeClosingTag(xmlText As String, tagName As String, insertion As String) As String
    Dim closeTag As String
    closeTag = "</" & tagName & ">"

    Dim p As Long
    p = InStrRev(xmlText, closeTag, -1, vbTextCompare)
    If p = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3105, "InjectRibbonXml", "Could not find closing tag " & closeTag & "."

    InsertBeforeClosingTag = Left$(xmlText, p - 1) & insertion & Mid$(xmlText, p)
End Function

Private Function RemoveXmlElementByAttribute(xmlText As String, elementName As String, attrName As String, attrValue As String) As String
    Dim searchValue As String
    searchValue = attrName & "=""" & attrValue & """"

    Dim p As Long
    p = InStr(1, xmlText, searchValue, vbTextCompare)

    Do While p > 0
        Dim startTag As Long
        startTag = InStrRev(Left$(xmlText, p), "<" & elementName, -1, vbTextCompare)
        If startTag = 0 Then Exit Do

        Dim endTag As Long
        endTag = InStr(p, xmlText, ">")
        If endTag = 0 Then Exit Do

        xmlText = Left$(xmlText, startTag - 1) & Mid$(xmlText, endTag + 1)
        p = InStr(1, xmlText, searchValue, vbTextCompare)
    Loop

    RemoveXmlElementByAttribute = xmlText
End Function

Private Function NextRelationshipId(relsXml As String) As String
    Dim n As Long
    n = 1
    Do While InStr(1, relsXml, "Id=""rIdCleanupSuite" & CStr(n) & """", vbTextCompare) > 0
        n = n + 1
    Loop
    NextRelationshipId = "rIdCleanupSuite" & CStr(n)
End Function

' ---------------------------
' End of ribbon setup module
' ---------------------------
